838. [Opis pilota] 臨海学園のサッカー部キャプテンを務めるスポーツ少年。
   ムートロンを研究していた父・一郎とムー帝国の皇女レムリアとの間に生まれ、ムー帝国の守護神であるライディーンにフェードインする事を運命づけられる。
   悪魔世紀の到来を防ぐため、ライディーンで妖魔帝国と戦う。
   熱血型の正義漢で性格も明るく、自らの宿命に負ける事のない強さと絶望的な状況にもひるまない勇気を持っている。
   しかし、生きていた母・レムリアへの想いから冷静さを欠く事もあった。
   limit: do 645 znaków
   EN: An athletic young man, captain of the Seaside Academy soccer team.
   
   Born from the union of Ichiro, a Mutron researcher, and Princess Lemuria of the Empire of Mu, he is destined to fuse with the guardian deity of Mu—i.e. to Fade In to Reideen.
   To protect the world against the coming of a new Age of Demons, he wields Reideen's power against the Yoma Empire's assaults.
   
   Energetic and hot-blooded, he has the strength of spirit to not falter under the weight of his destiny, and the courage to not flinch in the face of unbeatable odds.
   
   One of the few things to ever shake his poise is the revelation that his beloved mother, Lemuria, was alive.
   PL:

839. [Opis pilota] 洸の同級生で、臨海学園のアイドル的存在。
   未来工学研究所の東山所長の娘で、性格は明るくおてんば。そのおっちょこちょいぶりを洸によくからかわれていた。
   戦う洸の心の支えとなり、ついには洸の力になりたい一心でコープランダー隊に入り、ブルーガーでライディーンを援護した。
   limit: do 479 znaków
   EN: Akira's classmate and the idol of Seaside Academy. She is the daughter of Chief Higashiyama from the Future Engineering Research Institute, and has a bright, tomboyish personality. Her clumsiness often gets her teased by Akira.
   
   She is one of the pillars that supports Akira's heart in his fight and, wishing to fight by his side, she joins the Co-oplanders and pilots the Bluegar to aid Reideen.
   PL:

840. [Opis pilota] コープランダー隊の隊員で、超能力を持つ少女。
   鋭い霊感を持ち、ライディーンや化石獣を察知する事が出来る。
   かつては神宮寺力と共にブルーガーに乗り、ライディーンの戦いをサポートしていたが、洸の母・ひびき玲子の所在をつかむべく、ひびき一郎達と共にチベットへと向かった。
   コープランダー隊に入隊する以前は、暴走族に加わっていた時期がある他、シスターとして修道院に在籍していた過去も持つ。
   limit: do 479 znaków
   EN: A girl with psychic powers who is a member of the Co-oplanders. With her sharp psychic senses she is able to pinpoint the locations of Reideen and any Fossil Beasts.
   
   Though at first she rode on the Bluegar along with Chikara Shinguji to support Reideen, she later joined Ichiro Hibiki on his quest to Tibet to locate Akira's mother, Reiko Hibiki.
   
   Before joining the Co-oplanders she spent some time as a member of a motorcycle gang, as well as a period as a nun in a nunnery.
   PL:

841. [Opis pilota] ムトロポリスで技術開発を担当する少年。
   天才的な頭脳の持ち主で、メカの開発や解析を専門にしている。
   ムートロン研究にも携わり、ライディーンを解析する中でその秘められた力や武器等を発見した。
   コープランダー隊の一員として、ブルーガーに搭乗する事もあった。
   limit: do 479 znaków
   EN: A young man in charge of technological development in Mutropolis. He is a genius, with a special talent for creating and analyzing robots.
   
   His involvement with mutron research allowed him to analyze Reideen, discovering various hidden powers and weapons within the machine.
   
   As a member of the Co-oplanders, he also helped crew the Bluegar.
   PL:

842. [Opis pilota] 未来工学研究所の所長で、桜野マリの父。
   妖魔帝国の侵攻に対抗して、科学要塞ムトロポリスを建設。その指揮を執るかたわら、ライディーンをバックアップするため、コープランダー隊を結成した。
   limit: do 265 znaków
   EN: The head of the Future Engineering Research Institute, and father of Mari Sakurano. He built Mutropolis, a scientific fortress, in response to the invasion of the Yoma Empire.
   
   While acting as commander of Mutropolis, he also formed the Co-oplanders to aid Reideen.
   PL:

843. [Opis pilota] 考古学の博士で、洸の祖父。
   ムー大陸の古い記録から、悪魔の復活を予想していた。
   チベットで発見した古代ムー文明の粘土板を解読し、その歴史を紐解いた。
   limit: do 265 znaków
   EN: A professor of archeology and Akira's grandfather. He predicted the coming of the Yoma after reading ancient records from the continent of Mu.
   
   While in Tibet he deciphered tablets from the ancient Mu culture, unraveling the secrets of their history.
   PL:

844. [Opis pilota] 洸の母で古代ムー帝国の皇女。
   ムー帝国での名前はレムリア。
   １万２０００年前、遠い未来に復活する妖魔帝国の野望を阻止するため、眠りについた。
   現代に目覚め、ひびき一郎と結婚して洸を出産。その後、ムー帝国の秘宝「ラ・ムーの星」を探すために洸の前から姿を消した。
   そのため洸は、母が生きている事を知らなかった。
   limit: do 487 znaków
   EN: Akira's mother and empress of the ancient Mu Empire. In the Mu Empire her name was Lemuria.
   
   12,000 years ago she put herself in a suspended sleep to stop the ambitions of the revived Yoma Empire in the far future.
   
   She reawakened in the modern day, married Ichiro Hibiki and gave birth to Akira. Afterwards, she vanished from Akira's life as she went in search of the Star of Ra Mu, the Mu Empire's sacred treasure. Due to this, Akira was left unaware that his mother was still alive.
   PL:

845. [Opis pilota] 妖魔帝国との最終決戦が迫る中、洸を助けるために現れる。
   妖魔大帝バラオとの戦いでは、その生命と引き換えに「ラ・ムーの星」を発動させ、洸と人類を勝利に導いた。
   limit: do 395 znaków
   EN: She returns to Akira right before the final battle with the Yoma Empire.
   
   In the final fight against Yoma Emperor Barao she offers her own life in exchange for activating the Star of Ra Mu, which guides Akira and all of mankind to victory.
   PL:

846. [Opis pilota] 洸の父で考古学者。
   ムートロンの探索中、妖魔帝国によって石化されてしまう。
   シャーキンが倒れた後、石化から解放されて洸と無事再会を果たす。
   その後、行方不明の妻・玲子を探すため、久造と麗と共にチベットへと旅立った。
   limit: do 395 znaków
   EN: Akira's father and an archaeologist. While researching mutron energy, he was turned to stone by the Yoma Empire. However, he was released and safely reunited with Akira when Sharkin was defeated.
   
   Afterwards, he travels to Tibet with Kyuzo and Rei in search of his missing wife, Reiko.
   PL:

847. [Opis pilota] 妖魔帝国との最終決戦が迫る中、日本に帰国し、玲子とも再会を果たす。
   最後の戦いの後、玲子を失った洸を父として励ました。
   limit: do 395 znaków
   EN: With the final battle with the Yoma Empire looming, he returns to Japan and reunites with Reiko.
   
   After the final battle, he comforts his son Akira, who is struggling with the loss of Reiko.
   PL:

848. [Opis pilota] 妖魔帝国の支配者。
   １万２０００年の間、石像に封印されていたが、妖魔帝国最高司令官プリンス・シャーキンの生命を懸けた祈りによって復活を遂げた。
   悪魔世紀を実現するため、ムートロンを手に入れようとライディーンに幾度となく刺客を送る。
   ライディーンを遥かに凌ぐ巨体の持ち主で、下半身は妖魔島に埋もれている。
   limit: do 395 znaków
   EN: The ruler of the Yoma Empire.
   
   He was sealed away in a statue for 12,000 years but was freed thanks to the prayers and life Prince Sharkin, commander of the Yoma Empire, offered up to him.
   
   He desires mutron energy to usher in a new Age of Demons, so he sends countless assassins to destroy Reideen. He himself is far more massive than even Reideen, but his lower half is buried in Yoma Island.
   PL:

849. [Opis pilota] 全ての配下を失った後、その全魔力を使って天変地異を引き起こし、ムトロポリスへ進撃を開始した。
   ライディーンを寄せ付けないほどの圧倒的な力を見せるが、最期はラ・ムーの星によって巨大化したライディーンの前に敗れ去った。
   limit: do 395 znaków
   EN: Once all his underlings are crushed, he uses the full extent of his powers to bring about cataclysms and directly attack Mutropolis.
   
   Though at first even Reideen couldn't keep up with his overwhelming power, he is ultimately defeated when Reideen is made gigantic thanks to the Star of Ra Mu.
   PL:

850. [Opis pilota] 妖魔帝国の祭祀長。
   呪文を唱え、魔像から発せられる光線を利用して化石獣を生み出す。
   配下に複数の神官を抱えている。
   limit: do 196 znaków
   EN: The master of rituals for the Yoma Empire.
   
   By chanting incantations and harnessing the light rays of an evil statue he is able to create Fossil Beasts. He has countless priests under his command.
   PL:

851. [Opis pilota] 妖魔大帝バラオが復活した際、そこで生じた地割れに吞み込まれていった。
   limit: do 90 znaków
   EN: He was swallowed up by the earthquakes that heralded the awakening of Yoma Emperor Barao.
   PL:

852. [Opis pilota] バトルチームのリーダーで、１号機・バトルジェットのパイロットとコン・バトラーＶのメインパイロットを務める。
   両親を事故で失い、太陽学園という孤児院で育つ。
   オートバイの運転が得意でケンカっ早く激しい気性で、戦闘には天才的な才能を持っている。
   明るく単純であるが、「強きをくじき、弱きを助ける」の見本のような性格であり、ピンチに対しても諦めない闘志を持つ。しかし、女性に対しては全く奥手でちずるのアプローチになかなか反応しなかった。
   が、キャンベル星人との戦いが終わった後は気持ちを通わせ合うようになった。
   なお、その両腕は、宿敵ガルーダとの生身の戦いで撃たれた怪我により、高性能の義手を取り付けている。
   limit: do 763 znaków
   EN: The leader of the Battle Team. He is the pilot of the Unit 1, the Battlejet, and the main pilot of Combattler V.
   
   Having lost his parents as a child, he grew up at the "Sunny School" orphanage.
   
   He is a talented biker with a very short fuse, but his fighting skills are undeniable.
   
   His motto is "Help the weak and crush the strong". When faced with danger, he would rather die than give up the fight. However, he has a major shortcoming: he is incredibly dense when it comes to matters of the heart, and finds himself unable to fully understand his feelings for Chizuru until after the end of the war against the Campbellians.
   
   His arms were severely injured in combat against his archenemy Garuda, and had to be replaced with high-performance artificial limbs.
   PL:

853. [Opis pilota] バトルチームの一員で、２号機・バトルクラッシャーのパイロット。
   ニヒルでクールな性格で口も悪いが、関西弁で喋るため、あまり嫌味のない人物。
   豹馬とは時に意見をぶつけあい、殴りあいのケンカをすることもあるが、心の中では互いに仲間と認めあっている。
   ８歳で大会に優勝する程の天才的な射撃の腕前を持ち、その力でチームの危機を何度も救ってきた。コン・バトラーＶでも射撃武器の制御を担当している。
   limit: do 763 znaków
   EN: One of the members of the Battle Team. He is the pilot of Unit 2, the Battlecrasher.
   
   Calm and aloof, he always has a snarky remark for any given situation, though his Kansai accent makes him hard to really hate.
   
   He has a tendency to clash with Hyoma, both verbally and at times physically, but deep down they both consider each other irreplaceable comrades.
   
   He is a sharpshooting prodigy, winning tournaments since the age of 8; his skills saved the Battle Team on multiple occasions. He is also in charge of controlling Combattler V's firing systems.
   PL:

854. [Opis pilota] バトルチームの一員で、３号機・バトルタンクのパイロット。
   おっとりとしていて、気は優しくて力持ちタイプ。
   九州出身で漫画家を志望し、柔道の達人でもある。
   limit: do 255 znaków
   EN: One of the members of the Battle Team. He is the pilot of Unit 3, the Battletank.
   
   He is a gentle giant: big, strong, and with a kind heart.
   
   A highly skilled judo practitioner born and raised in Kyushu, his dream is to become a professional manga artist.
   PL:

855. [Opis pilota] バトルチームの紅一点で、４号機・バトルマリンのパイロット。

   EN: A teenage Newtype and pilot of the Zeta Gundam with the AEUG. 17 years old.
   
   He started out as a somewhat sensitive but otherwise ordinary student going to high school on the space colony Green Noa. He had a complex about his name, Kamille, being traditionally a girl's name, so he threw himself into more "masculine" pursuits like flying homoavis (a simple type of glider) and karate. He has a familiarity with mechanics due to his parents (both Earth Federation tech officers), allowing him to win a Junior Mobile Suit Competition.
   
   After Jerid Messa mocked his name, he got into a series of fights and mishaps that radicalized him against the Titans. Over the course of these events, he helped Quattro's team in their theft of the Gundam Mk-II and ended up joining the AEUG.
   
   As he continued fighting for the AEUG, latent Newtype abilities awoke within him, and eventually, he rose to the position of the AEUG's top ace pilot.
   
   At first he fought out of antipathy for the Titans, but he grew as a person over the course of his encounters with adult figures like Quattro and Amuro and women like Four Murasame and Rosamia Badam. As he sought a way to end the war, his consciousness reached out into space.
   
   Accounts of his final fate differ: in the TV series finale, he kills Scirocco, only for Scirocco to launch a final mental assault as he dies, essentially "taking Kamille with him" and leaving Kamille with severe mental damage. This was changed in the finale of the movie edition, in which he and Fa both safely found each other.

   Underneath his unsociable and cold demeanor is a passionate and kind heart.

   EN: An Operation Meteor agent sent from L5 Colony Cluster to Earth, he is 16 years old.
   
   He comes from a line of warriors from ancient China. He believes that strength is justice, and was raised as a warrior. He regards the OZ's actions of oppressing the weak with power as evil, and fights under the belief that justice is defeating them with greater strength.
   
   When he was bested by Treize in a physical fight, he realized how powerless he was and lost all will to fight. He was struck by how Sally Po recognized her own weakness, yet still continued to fight. He came to believe that never giving up is the way to change the world, and regained his will to fight. He went up against Treize once more in the final battle and defeated him this time, but it was an incomprehensible conclusion that felt as though Treize was quitting while ahead.
   
   After the battle, the world returned to peace and people forgot about the state of war. The warriors who lived on the battlefield had no place to go, causing Wufei to question whether there was justice in this world. He arrived at the conclusion that this type of world was evil, and joined the Mariemaia Army.

   EN: The United Earth Sphere Nation's Vice Foreign Minister, 16 years old.
   
   She is the princess of the former Sanc Kingdom that advocated total pacifism, and her real name is Relena Peacecraft. She was raised as the daughter of Vice Minister Darlian. When he was assassinated, she learned of her real identity, and resolved to take on her real father's vision for total pacifism and re-establish the Sanc Kingdom.
   
   The kingdom was destroyed by OZ's invasion, but she became a leader of Earth and all colonies due to the influence of the Romefeller Foundation. Even as she was tossed around by others' political motives, she stuck fast to her belief of total pacifism, envisioning a world without arms and violence.

   最終決戦の際にもアスランの説得を受けるが、かたくなに戦いを続ける。錯乱のあまり、心を通い合わせていたルナマリアにすら攻撃をしかけそうになるが、あわやというところをアスランに撃墜され、幻想の中でステラと再会。ルナマリアの膝の上で目覚め、涙の中で戦いの終焉を迎えた。
   『ファイナルプラス』で追加された後日談では、オーブの慰霊碑を訪れた際、２度目となるキラとの対面を果たす。そして、平和を維持する戦いを共に続けるという誓いの握手を交わした。

   EN: A Mobile Suit pilot with ZAFT's Minerva. 16 years old.
   
   Born and raised on Orb, Shinn had led a peaceful life, blissfully unaware of the fighting that would soon come. During the invasion of Orb by the Earth Alliance, he watched as his parents and little sister were killed in front of him.
   
   Soon after, he moves to PLANT at the recommendation of Todaka, an Orb soldier. He joins ZAFT, motivated by his desire to become strong enough to protect that which is important to him. He graduates with excellent grades from the Academy, wearing a red uniform to show that he is an elite pilot. Shinn is lively but can be quite naive. There are times when he struggles to contain his aggression.
   
   Seeing the boy's potential, Dullindal expects great things from him and assigns Shinn to the cutting-edge Impulse Gundam. When three second-stage Gundam are stolen, he scrambles to retrieve them.
   
   Initially, Shinn's lack of combat experience holds him back, but with repeated fighting and a strong desire to live, SEED mode awakens in him. His skills develop into those of an ace pilot, and he fights with intense passion. Although Shinn respects Athrun, he often gets angry at his non-committal attitude to things.
   
   Shinn deems Kira to be his mortal enemy after the latter kills Stellar, leading him to take down Kira and the Freedom Gundam.
   
   Shinn then attacks Athrun, who has defected from Dullindal. He is rewarded with the Destiny Gundam and an appointment to Dullindal’s vanguard, the secret corps, FAITH.
   
   In the final battle, Athrun attempts to convince Shinn to stop fighting, but is unable to make him change his mind. In his deluded state, Shinn even almost attacks Lunamaria, someone who he previously shared a deep bond with. Fortunately, he is shot down by Athrun just in time, and whilst unconscious, Shinn hallucinates that he is reunited with Stellar. He wakes up with his head in Lunamaria's lap, and he cannot hold back his tears as he watches the closing of the war.
   
   In the sequel "Final Plus", he visits the memorial at Orb and once again comes face to face with Kira. This time, however, the two shake hands and vow to fight together to maintain the peace.

   EN: A Mobile Suit pilot with ZAFT's Minerva. 17 years old.

921. [Opis pilota] 前大戦を終結に導く要因となった１人で、プラントでその名を知らない者はいない程の英雄的存在。１８歳。
   終戦後はオーブへ亡命し、恋人であるカガリの護衛役を務めていた。だが、再び始まった戦争の前に、何もできない無力さを噛み締めていた。そこをデュランダルに誘われ、カガリに別れを告げてザフトへ復隊。特務隊ＦＡＩＴＨの一員となった。
   復隊時に託されたセイバーガンダムを駆り、ミネルバの搭乗員となって戦うも、やがて見解の相違から親友であるキラと対立してしまう。
   当初はデュランダルの理念に共感していたものの、ラクスの暗殺疑惑や偽者であるミーアを利用していた事、さらに志は同じであるはずのアークエンジェルへの攻撃命令などを受けて、徐々に不信感を募らせていった。
   その後、ザフトから脱走し、再びキラ達と共に戦うようになり、デュランダルの提唱する「デスティニープラン」に立ち向かっていった。
   最終決戦の際には、デュランダルに従うシンに対して、戦いを止めるよう説得するが失敗。錯乱したシンが制止に入ったルナマリアに対しても攻撃する姿勢を見せたため、ＳＥＥＤを発現させて撃墜した。
   limit: do 1478 znaków
   EN: One of the people who helped bring about the end of the previous war, everyone aboard the PLANTs knows Athrun as a hero. He is 18 years old.
   
   After the war ended, Athrun seeks refuge in Orb, where he becomes a bodyguard for his lover, Cagalli. When war begins once more, he finds himself entirely powerless, until one day when he receives an invitation to return from Dullindal. After saying his goodbyes to Cagalli, he returns to ZAFT to become a member of the special corps, FAITH.
   
   Upon his return, Athrun pilots the Savior Gundam, and fights as part of the Minerva crew. Unfortunately, a difference of opinion leads him to fall out with his best friend, Kira.
   
   Although he initially agreed with Dullindal's vision, Athrun begins to distrust him after Dullindal is accused of attempting to assassinate Lacus, seeing how he uses Meer, and after he finds himself ordered to attack Archangel, who was on their side.
   
   Athrun decides to escape from ZAFT, and after reconciling with his friend Kira, the two fight together to stop Dullindal's Destiny Plan.
   
   During the final battle, Athrun tries to persuade Shinn to leave Dullindal, but he is unable to convince him to stop fighting. When Shinn even tries to attack Lunamaria as she tried to stop the two fighting, Athrun enters SEED mode in order to shoot him down.
   PL:

922. [Opis pilota] アークエンジェルの乗員で、ストライクフリーダムガンダムのパイロット。１８歳。
   遺伝子調整に加え、様々な実験を重ねた結果誕生した「スーパーコーディネイター」とでもいうべき存在。
   前大戦時は、第三勢力として地球連合とプラントの双方を相手に戦い抜き、戦争を終結に導いた。その後、共に戦ったマリューや、恋人であるラクスと共にオーブに亡命。戦いとは無縁の世界で隠棲していた。しかし、ユニウスセブンの落下事件を目の当たりにした事、そしてラクス暗殺部隊との一戦を契機に、拡大していく戦火を食い止める必要性を強く感じ始める。そしてついに、かつての仲間達と共に新たな戦いを開始した。
   戦闘能力は他者の追従を許さないほどに高い。にもかかわらず争いや人殺しを好まない優しすぎる性格のため、戦闘となっても武器やカメラだけを破壊して相手を殺さない戦い方を心がけている。やがてそれが仇となり、シンの捨て身の攻撃に一度は敗れてしまう。だが、のちに新たな翼、ストライクフリーダムガンダムを得て復活を果たした。
   デュランダルが「デスティニープラン」の存在を明らかにした後は、カガリの代表首長復帰に伴い正式にオーブ軍に所属している。最終決戦ではラウ・ル・クルーゼの因縁を引き継ぐレイと決着をつけると、メサイアに潜入してデュランダルと対峙。混迷の世界で戦い続ける覚悟を示した。

   EN: A crew member of the Archangel and a pilot of the Strike Freedom Gundam, at 18 years old, Kira is the result of various experiments and gene modifications. He is known as the "Ultimate Coordinator".
   
   During the previous war, he fought both the Earth Alliance and the PLANTs as a third force, and helped bring about the end of the war. Later, Kira traveled to Orb with his comrade, Murrue, and his lover, Lacus, and they remained there for a while, secluded from the fighting. However, after witnessing the Junius Seven incident and battling the troops who came to assassinate Lacus, the three decide that they must stop the war from consuming anything else. Kira steps once more onto the battlefield alongside his comrades.
   
   Although Kira's combat prowess is unsurpassed, at heart he remains a gentle soul who abhors fighting and killing. Even in battle, he will only destroy weapons or cameras, in order to avoid lethally injuring his enemies. His kindness turns out to be his weakness, however, and he loses once to a desperate attack from Shinn. Despite his loss, he is able to make a comeback when he gains the Strike Freedom Gundam.
   
   After Dullindal reveals the Destiny Plan, Cagalli returns to act as representative and Kira formally joins the Orb troops. In the final battle, he faces Rey, who has inherited the fate of Rau Le Creuset. Kira infiltrates Messiah and confronts Dullindal, demonstrating that he is prepared to continue the fight for good in a world of chaos.

923. [Opis pilota] オーブ連合首長国の代表。１８歳。
   かつての代表である父ウズミの遺志を継いで、祖国と世界を平和に導こうとする。しかしその若さや経験不足、セイラン親子の暗躍もあって国がまとまらず、自身の力のなさを痛感する事になる。
   前大戦の折から恋仲であったアスランがオーブを離れる際、指輪を贈られている。だがその後、「国を１つにまとめるため」という理由から、やむなくユウナとの結婚を決意し、想いを断ち切ってしまう。しかし、結婚式の最中に双子の兄弟キラのフリーダムガンダムに連れ去られた事で結婚は御破算となった。
   その後、実権を奪ったセイラン家が地球連合と同盟を結んで軍を動かし始めたため、それを阻止しようと働きかけるも失敗。だが、オーブ軍人達の決死の覚悟を目の当たりにした事で、祖国を守るという決意を新たにした。さらにオーブに危機が訪れた際には、ウズミの遺したモビルスーツ・アカツキを駆って祖国の危機を救った。
   再びオーブ代表となった後は国をまとめ上げ、「デスティニープラン」を提唱するデュランダルに立ち向かった。

   EN: Representative of the Orb United Emirates. 18 years old, she took over the position from her late father Uzumi and has been trying to lead her country and the world to peace as per his wishes. However, she is unable to keep her people united due to her age, lack of experience, and the plotting of the Seirans, making her keenly aware of her powerlessness.
   
   When Athrun—her lover since the last war—leaves Orb, she gives him a ring. However, she later breaks off the relationship when she finds herself with no choice but to marry Yuna in order to unite Orb as one. However, her twin brother Kira interrupts the wedding with his Freedom Gundam and takes her away, annulling the marriage.
   
   Afterwards, when the Seirans steal control of Orb and begin deploying their military after joining forces with the Earth Alliance, she attempts to stop them, but fails. However, seeing the do-or-die resolve of Orb's soldiers inspires her own determination to protect her nation.
   
   When Orb finds itself in even further danger, she saves it using the Akatsuki Mobile Suit left behind by Uzumi. After regaining her position as Orb's representative, she reunites her people and opposes Dullindal and his Destiny Plan proposal.

924. [Opis pilota] かつて「プラントの歌姫」と呼ばれたアイドル的存在。１８歳。
   その存在感とカリスマ性は絶大で、前大戦ではキラやアスラン達に道を指し示し、志を同じくする者達を集めるなど、戦乱を終結に導く大きな原動力となっていた。

   さらに偽者であるミーアの出現もあって、デュランダルへの疑念を強める。やがて表舞台に戻る事を決意したラクスは、キラと共にアークエンジェルに乗り込んだ。

   最終決戦の際には、前大戦同様にエターナルに乗り込み、最前線で旗印としての役割を果たした。

   EN: A former pop star known as the "Singing Princess of the PLANTs". She is 18 years old.
   
   Lacus has a real sense of presence and charisma. In the previous war, she was a major factor in bringing the war to an end by directing Kira and Athrun, and also by rallying together like-minded people.
   
   After the war, she went to Orb with her comrades Waltfeld, Murrue, and her lover, Kira. There, she lives a quiet life with Reverend Malchio, Kira's mother-in-law, Calida, and the orphans. However, an assassination attempt on her life breaks her temporary respite.
   
   With the emergence of Meer, her imposter, she is increasingly suspicious of Dullindal. Lacus decides to return to the stage and boards the Archangel with Kira.
   
   Later, Lacus goes into space to fight, too, and when she learns of the Destiny Plan, she announces her intention to oppose it.
   
   When new units are ready for Kira and Athrun, she pilots the Infinite Justice Gundam in order to deliver it to Athrun. She has a lot of energy, throwing herself into her battles. Lacus becomes a key support figure, both spiritually and ideologically, for those who disagree with Dullindal.
   
   In the final battle, Lacus is on board the Eternal, as in the previous war, where she acts as a banner for the front line. When the battle with Dullindal is over, Lacus and Kira decide to go public about their relationship.

   元はザフトに所属し、「砂漠の虎」の異名で恐れられていた猛者であった。だが、前大戦の最中に軍を離反し、ラクス達に協力し、戦乱を終結に導いた。

   プラントへの移住を考えていた折、ラクス暗殺を目論む特殊部隊の襲撃を受け、これを退ける。それをきっかけとして、再び第三勢力として、戦争と向かい合う事を決意した。
   アークエンジェル発進後は副艦長的な立場でマリューを補佐しつつ、有事の際にはパーソナルカラーに染めたムラサメで出撃し、未だ衰えていない腕前を披露。やがてラクスがプラントの現状を確認するために行動を起こした際には、その護衛として共に宇宙に上がっている。

   EN: Captain of the high-speed warship, the Eternal. He is 32 years old.
   
   Originally a member of ZAFT, Waltfeld was known for his nickname, "Desert Tiger", and was a feared veteran. However, during the previous war, he defected to join Lacus, a move which helped bring about an end to the fighting.

   EN: A member of the military-industrial complex "Logos" and a new leader of the Blue Cosmos, who aim to exterminate all Coordinators.
   
   He is 31 years old and an elitist who is excessively preoccupied with nobility. Although normally calm and courteous, he can quickly become furious when things don't go his way, and if provoked too far, he will take his rage out on anything or anyone. He can also be very crass.

   However, the Aerial is suspected of being a GUND-ARM, which gets her wrapped up in conflicts both within the school and with the wider Benerit Group. Suletta wins duel after duel against the pilots of the Benerit Group's Three Branches by using the Aerial to its fullest potential, defending her position as Miorine's fiancée candidate.
   PL:

933. [Opis pilota] ベネリットグループの総裁であるデリング・レンブランの一人娘。
   アスティカシア高等専門学園経営戦略科２年生で、その成績は経営戦略科内でトップ。父親の威光と容姿端麗である事も合わせて一部からは「お姫様」と揶揄されている。
   一方的に物事を決定する父に強い反発心を持っており、学園から脱出して地球へ行く事を願っている。
   父の定めたルールにより、１７歳になった時点で「ホルダー」の地位にある人物と結婚する事が決められている。
   学園の敷地内で亡き母が作った品種のトマトを栽培しており、その温室を心の拠り所としている。
   limit: do 684 znaków
   EN: The only daughter of Delling Rembran, president of the Benerit Group. A second-year management strategy student at Asticassia School of Technology, her grades are at the top of the management strategy department. Some teasingly refer to her as "princess" due to her beauty and her father's position of authority.
   
   In truth, she despises her father for making decisions about her life entirely on his own, and wishes to escape the school to go to Earth. As per her father's rules, she is to marry the one who holds the title of Holder when she turns seventeen.
   
   Grows a breed of tomatoes created by her late mother in a greenhouse on school grounds, which keep her going in hard times.
   PL:

934. [Opis pilota] グエルに勝利してホルダーとなったスレッタを利用して父の定めたルールからの逃走を図るが、スレッタの真っ直ぐさに徐々に心を開いていく。
   ガンダム・エアリアルをめぐってスレッタが窮地に立たされた際には「株式会社ガンダム」の事業提案を行い、父の信用を借りて出資を集める事に成功した。
   その後は、地球寮のメンバーを巻き込みながら、社長として株式会社ガンダムの経営に奔走した。
   limit: do 684 znaków
   EN: Once Suletta won the title of Holder from Guel, she hatched a plan to use the technical letter of her father's rules to escape the yoke she found herself placed under. Suletta's sincerity and earnestness, however, made her slowly open her heart and wonder if there might be more to her "plan" than she imagined.
   
   Proposes the forming of GUND-ARM Inc. when Suletta finds herself in hot water over the Gundam Aerial, and succeeds in borrowing her father's trust to raise funds. Afterwards, she runs GUND-ARM Inc. as its president while bringing the members of Earth House on board with the idea of the company.
   PL:

   EN: A third-year piloting student at Asticassia School of Technology and Jeturk House's ace pilot. Son of Vim Jeturk, CEO of Jeturk Heavy Machinery, one of the Benerit Group's Three Branches.
   
   Initially possesses the title of Holder—the top pilot in the school—and is the head of the Dueling Committee. A ruffian with a penchant for bullying others due to his overconfidence. Dreams of one day becoming the ace pilot for the Dominicus Unit—Cathedra's personal special forces.
   PL:

   敗北を重ねた事で父・ヴィムの怒りを買い、さらにはエラン・ケレスとの決闘で敗れた事からジェターク寮から退寮させられた。

   Is known by some students as the Ice Prince for his lack of interest in others and cold personality.

938. [Opis pilota] その正体は本物のエラン・ケレスと同じ容姿に整形された影武者であり、ペイル社では「強化人士４号」と呼ばれている。
   「GUND-ARM」搭乗のために身体改造を施されており、自身と同じ境遇にあると考えたスレッタに興味を示す。しかし、スレッタが自身と異なる境遇であると知ると彼女に決闘を申し込んだ。
   スレッタに敗北するが、自分にはないものと思っていた幸せな過去を思い出す。しかし、身体の限界から与えられた役目を果たせなくなり、その任務から降ろされた。

   EN: He is, in fact, a body double who has been made to look like the real Elan Ceres via plastic surgery and is referred to as "Enhanced Person #4" by Peil Technologies.
   
   His body has been modified to allow him to pilot a GUND-ARM, and he takes an interest in Suletta under the belief she is the same in that regard. But as soon as he realizes she isn't, he challenges her to a duel.
   
   He loses to Suletta, but remembers a happy past when he initially believed there was nothing to him at all. However, he was removed from the mission by Peil Technologies after his body reached its limits and he could no longer fulfill his duty.
   
   Afterwards, he was replaced by "Enhanced Person #5".
   PL:

   当初は状況に流されドレイクの味方をしていたが、マーベルとの出会いを経て、自らの意志で反ドレイク側に回る。その高いオーラ力からすぐに反ドレイク側勢力の中核を担い始め、味方からは聖戦士として頼られるようになる。
   本人はそういった扱いを面映ゆく思っている事が多かったが、戦いが進むに連れ徐々に自分の役割を認識し、戦士として、ニーの片腕としてその役割を全うし始める。
   経営コンサルタントの父と教育評論家の母を持つが、初めて地上へ帰還した際に再会した彼らはショウの生き方を認めることができずに決別。以降、ショウは地上への未練を捨て、バイストン・ウェルを自分の居場所と認識するようになる。
   全てのオーラマシンが地上へ展開してからは、地上にまで広がってしまったバイストン・ウェルの戦いを止める為に奔走する。

   He was an aspiring motocross rider but became the Dunbine's pilot after being summoned to Byston Well by the E Ferario, Silky Mau.
   
   Caught up in his new situation, he was initially Drake's ally, but switched to the anti-Drake force after meeting Marvel and starting to think on his own. His high Aura Power proved valuable, and he immediately became the core of the anti-Drake forces, gaining the trust of his new allies as a Battler. He himself is embarrassed being treated as such, but gradually accepts his role as the battle progresses, embracing his role as a Battler and as Nie's right-hand man.
   
   His father is a management consultant while his mother is an education critic. When he first returned to Upper Earth and reunited with his parents, they were unable to accept that he was still alive, and they parted ways. After that, Show threw away all his attachments to Upper Earth, making a place for himself in Byston Well.
   
   Eventually, Aura Machines and the war in Byston Well spread to Upper Earth. Show went berserk in order to stop everything.

   EN: L-Gaim's Headliner (its pilot).
   
   A young man from the planet Coam, Daba ventured forth from his hometown alongside his friend Mirawoo Kyao to enlist in Posaydal's regular army.
   
   Don't be deceived by his gentle facial features—Daba has a fiercely stubborn side to him, with a tendency to stop listening once he gets going.
   
   Nonetheless, his honesty, kindness, and powerful sense of determination were a magnet for trustworthy allies.
   
   He was also infamous as a magnet for the opposite sex, but typically refused to express his feelings in return, saying that he "had no right to determine the outcome of someone else's life"—a policy which tended to inspire jealousy.
   
   Daba's adventures, beginning with a simple task to deliver a 1-million-gheen credit chip, eventually led him to join the rebels. As a rebel, he fought against Posaydal's standing army in the handmade A Class Heavy Metal L-Gaim, a memento of his father.
   PL:

   EN: After a series of trials and tribulations, he ultimately became the leader of the rebellion.
   
   In fact, the name Daba Myroad was itself a fabrication. His true identity was that of Camen Myroad, heir to the planet Mizun's Yaman Dynasty, which met its end at Posaydal's hands.
   
   Raised by royal retainer Daba Hatzer, he grew up alongside Quwasan Olibee, who ultimately became his fiancée. When Olibee went missing, Daba set off from his hometown to find her.
   
   Over the course of many battles, Daba upgraded the L-Gaim to the L-Gaim Mark-II, guided the populace of Pentagona World as leader of the rebellion, and ultimately brought down the true Oldna Posaydal.
   
   With the war over, Daba declined a key position in the new government, opting instead to return to Coam to look after a mentally distraught Olibee.
   PL:

   EN: A survivor of the Mirali, one of Pentagona's indigenous peoples.
   
   After Daba rescued her from a circus, Lilis joins his crew. Though unwilling to open up to humans, she grew more comfortable around them in time, eventually forming an emotional bond with Daba and the others.
   
   The Mirali were pacifists at heart, but the human world seemed to rub off on Lilis, who often found herself shocked at her willingness to see foes driven before the team.
   
   A skilled cook and seamstress, Lilis had an extensive wardrobe of her own creation, and sometimes appeared in pajamas or a black sneaking suit.
   
   Her Lilliputian size and ability to fly made her indispensable for recon and infiltration assignments, and she always tagged along with Daba in the cockpit of his Heavy Metal.
   PL:

   EN: A soldier in Posaydal's regular forces.
   
   Hailing from one of the less-successful aristocratic families in Coam's countryside, Gablae set off with a used Allone to enlist in the army, the village's hopes riding on his shoulders. When he stole Daba's lunch along the way, an eternal rivalry was born.
   
   Incredibly self-confident, Gablae was always looking to improve his station in life, but his attempts to look cool frequently backfired. He was easy to impress, had a childish side to him, and fell in love at the drop of a hat...but for all his character flaws, it was and is nearly impossible to hate the gormless goober.
   
   A skilled Headliner, he ended up in a key position after a series of transfers, and was ultimately entrusted with an A Class Heavy Metal.
   
   His hometown believed the Mirali to be demonic familiars, leaving him with a deep-seated prejudice against Lilis.

1167. [Opis pilota] かつて悪逆皇帝として、世界征服を果たし、世界中の憎しみを一身に集めた少年。
   しかし、それは力による憎しみの連鎖が続く世界を壊し、武器を持たないすべての人が話し合いによって紡がれる世界を創るための所業だった。
   世界の三分の一を支配する超大国、神聖ブリタニア帝国の第１１皇子として生まれたルルーシュだったが、母を暗殺され、最愛の妹ナナリーが不具になったことで、人質として日本へと送られる。そこで生涯の親友であり宿敵となる枢木スザクと出会い、ささやかな安寧の日々を送るが、父であるシャルル皇帝が行った日本への武力侵攻でその日々さえ失ってしまう。
   戦場となった日本でルルーシュは、皇族の証であるブリタニア姓を捨てて母の姓を名乗り、ブリタニアの植民地と化した日本で妹のナナリーと共に身を潜めて暮らしていた。
   祖国ブリタニアへの復讐を誓うルルーシュは、偶然出会った少女Ｃ．Ｃ．から「絶対遵守の力」のギアスを得、仮面の男ゼロとしてブリタニアへの反逆を開始する。その結果、ルルーシュは自ら作り出した正義の象徴たるゼロに、憎しみのすべてを一身に受けた自分が殺されることこそが、妹ナナリーが安心して暮らせる世界を創り出すという考えに至る。そして、スザクにゼロを託し、自らが殺されることで、平和な世界を創り出すゼロ・レクイエムを完遂した。
   全ての人々の憎しみを一身に受けた悪逆皇帝ルルーシュが正義の象徴たるゼロに殺されることで憎しみの連鎖を断ち切ったゼロ・レクイエム。ルルーシュとスザクという２人の少年によって行われたこの計画により、世界はようやく平和な時代を手に入れた。
   ゼロ・レクイエムから一年、人類史上において戦争もテロもない「奇蹟の明日」と呼ばれる時代、死んだはずのルルーシュの身体は、彼の戦いの中で引き継がれてしまった「コード」によって蘇生されてしまう。正常なコードの引継ぎが行われていたならＣ．Ｃ．と同様の不老不死になるはずだったが、Ｃの世界が異常を引き起こしたことによって、ルルーシュの魂はＣの世界に取り残されたままとなってしまった。
   Ｃ．Ｃ．は、そんなルルーシュの心を取り戻すために、世界各地の遺跡を巡る旅に出た。
   limit: do 3808 znaków
   EN: His true name is Lelouch vi Britannia.
   
   The man who dragged the world into the depths of chaos as the Emperor of the Holy Britannian Empire, eventually slain by the leader of the Black Knights, Zero, and believed dead by the whole world.
   
   Formerly a high school-level student at the privately held Ashford Academy, his true identity was the eleventh prince of the Holy Britannian Empire, seventeenth in line for the Imperial throne, Lelouch vi Britannia.
   
   During the Shinjuku Incident, Lelouch encountered C.C., and in return for promising to grant her wish, he gained a Geass with the absolute power to command. He would use this power in a rebellion against the world, seeking to create a safe world for his sister, Nunnally, where she could live happily and in peace.
   
   Possessing both a brilliant mind and an astounding eye for strategy, along with the new power of his Geass, Lelouch was able to absorb the various resistance groups in Area 11 (Britannian-occupied Japan) into one group, forming the Black Knights and taking the role of their leader, "Zero". From there he slowly built up his forces until he was able to fight on an equal footing with the Britannian forces.
   
   However, in the decisive battle for the liberation of Japan, the Black Rebellion, he lost Nunnally, his entire motive for fighting, to an abduction, and abandoned the battlefield. Searching for Nunnally, he would make his way to Kamine Island, where he faced his close friend, Suzaku. This ended in the total rejection of his existence, with his identity fully revealed, and he was dragged before the Emperor of Britannia.
   
   There he learned his father, Charles, was also a Geass user, and by that Geass his memories would be overwritten. He was returned to a time before he was Zero, and implanted with memories of being the scion of lower nobility.
   
   This peace would last but a year, before Kallen and C.C.'s return would bring it to an end.
   
   Lelouch regained his memories courtesy of C.C. and would return to being Zero. Eventually, through multiple schemes and strategies, he would form a unified alliance, the United Federation of Nations, to oppose Britannia.
   
   During this battle, however, Lelouch would lose C.C. (who in turn had lost her memories and was no longer the previous C.C.) and Nunnally, ultimately further losing his position with the Black Knights due to his brother Schneizel's own plot, and then during his escape lost Rolo, the assassin-turned-friend by his side, as well.
   
   With nothing left to lose, Lelouch charged into C's World, believing he would never return, and faced off against his father...and his deceased mother. His parents explained to him their plan, to unify all human consciousness, which he rejected in totality. Together with C.C., who had regained her memories, he erased his father and mother from existence entirely.
   
   Having claimed victory over Schneizel and the Black Knights, Lelouch held the entire world in his hand, but was finally slain by Suzaku, in the guise of Zero.
   
   However, this was all a plot by Suzaku, Lelouch, and their co-conspirators, who wanted nothing more than to build a new future. This plot, which would be known as the Zero Requiem, concluded with Lelouch's death, leading the world to a new age.
   
   Lelouch's corpse would be smuggled out in secret, and reconstructed by C.C. on her own initiative. She gambled on Lelouch having inherited the Code from Charles Zi Britannia, making him functionally immortal. However, C's World, the source of power for the Code, had become unstable when Lelouch killed the collective unconsciousness. As a result, though his body was in fact still alive, his mind (including his will and memories) did not return, making him, in C.C.'s own words, a void inside Lelouch's body, the last traces of who he once was.

   EN: A girl with the Code, the power that grants the Geass and also gives its possessor an immortal body. In her quest for death, she gave Lelouch his Geass and followed him in hopes that she could pass her Code on to him. However, she had a change of heart as she watched Lelouch fight for a better future.
   
   As his accomplice, she bore witness to Lelouch' death as part of the Zero Requiem. Because Lelouch retained his Geass power after taking the Code of Charles' backer, V.V., she assumed he couldn't bear a Code, an assumption proven false by his physical resurrection after the Zero Requiem.
   
   However, only his body had resurrected, leaving him in a childlike state. Under the theory that his mind was trapped in the World of C, she took him on a journey to ruins that could allow them to access it. This journey finally brought them to Adam's Gate in the Kingdom of Zilkhstan.

1172. [Opis pilota] ルルーシュの親友であり、最大の敵。
   ルルーシュとは幼少期に出会って以来親友となったが、神聖ブリタニア帝国が日本に侵攻した際に生き別れとなってしまう。
   戦争を終わらせようと日本最後の首相であった父ゲンブを殺害したことから、その罪に悩まされており、「間違った方法で手に入れた結果に意味はない」という信念を持つようになる。そのため、ブリタニアを内部から変えようとブリタニア軍に入隊。そこでルルーシュと再会する。しかし、お互いに素性を明かさないまま、ゼロとなったルルーシュと幾度も対峙し、その結果、最愛の女性であるユーフェミアを失ってしまう。ユーフェミアの死により、結果を追い求めることになったスザクは、ルルーシュをシャルル皇帝に差し出すことで帝国最強の騎士ナイトオブラウンズにまで上りつめる。しかし、その行動の結果、大量破壊兵器フレイヤを使用して多くの人命を奪うことになり、その罪を贖うためにルルーシュとゼロ・レクイエムを決行する。
   ゼロ・レクイエムによりゼロという英雄の仮面を被り続けることになったスザクは、自らの人生を捨ててゼロとして世界の平和のために従事する。
   難民キャンプへの視察に向かったナナリーの護衛を務めるが、シャリオの罠にはまり、ナナリーとともにジルクスタン王国に囚われてしまう。
   limit: do 1574 znaków
   EN: Lelouch's best friend and worst enemy.
   
   He and Lelouch were close friends when they were children, but their lives went in drastically different directions when the Holy Britannian Empire invaded Japan.
   
   He assassinated his father, Genbu, the last prime minister of Japan, in an effort to put an end to the war. Haunted by his own terrible misdeed, he convinced himself that the ends never justifies the means, and this belief led him to join the Britannian military in hopes of changing Britannia from the inside.
   
   He eventually came back into contact with Lelouch, with neither of them revealing their true identity to the other. He fought Lelouch—masquerading as Zero—several times, until ultimately, Lelouch's struggles took the life of Euphemia, Lelouch's older sister and the love of Suzaku's life. Euphemia's death drove Suzaku to once again prioritize the ends over the means, and by capturing Lelouch for Emperor Charles, he secured himself a spot within the Knights of the Round, the greatest knights of Britannia.
   
   His actions led to the use of the F.L.E.I.J.A., a weapon of mass destruction, resulting in innumerable deaths. To atone for his sins, he resolved to work with Lelouch to carry out the Zero Requiem.
   
   In the Zero Requiem, Suzaku donned the mask of a hero and took on the identity of Zero. He gave everything up in order to live as Zero and serve the cause of world peace.
   
   He was in charge of security for Nunnally when she visited a refugee camp, but fell for a trap set by Shalio, leading to both him and Nunnally being taken prisoner by Zilkhstan.

   During the Black Rebellion, also known as the First Battle of Tokyo, she discovered Zero's true identity. However, he won her faith by standing to fight for Nunnally, and she continued to fight for him in the Guren Flight-Type and the Guren S.E.I.T.E.N. Eight Elements.
   
   However, she never learned Lelouch's intentions during the Zero Requiem until she was forced to watch him die.    EN: A member of Farlaf, which branched off from the Geass Order, and the possessor of a Geass of endless rebirth. Her Geass allows her to effectively travel back in time from the moment of her death, with memories intact; which she uses to return to the past to create "prophecies".

   She set out to change the world, as her country, the Kingdom of Zilkhstan, found its primary export—its mercenary companies—to be worthless in the peace created by the Zero Requiem.    Ultimately she is placed in an eternal sleep by Lelouch's Geass.

   EN: A special forces operative called a NINJA (New Infinity Neuron Japanese Assassin), based on the ancient Japanese ninja, and leader (or "head") of the Special Stealth Forces NINJA group known as Haze.
   
   Age 21, male.
   
   He puts the mission first and doesn't allow his feelings to interfere with the job; he'll even go so far as to adopt completely false identities to finish a mission. As a result, he's sometimes considered cold and even heartless by those around him.
   
   He is the strongest NINJA in the history of Haze. He is not only skilled at Ninja Arts and Ninja Fangs but possesses an amazing mastery of Mobile Armored Ninja piloting.
   
   He also has complete mental control over his body, which gives him a tremendous resistance to poisons and toxins.

   EN: The daughter of Yagami City's primary landholder.
   
   Age 14, female.
   
   Her mother died when she was young, leaving her to live alone in an enormous mansion in Yagami City together with her teacher Annina and her ninety-nine maids. While she never attended a formal school, she's received a wide variety of lessons from Annina to prepare her to be the next governor of Yagami City. This has left her well educated in not just politics and economics but even military affairs.
   
   She takes everything she does seriously and throws herself into it, and hates making a poor showing of herself. While she can be somewhat clumsy when communicating with others due to her lack of experience, she acts like the young girl she is at heart when she is with the people she's grown close to.

1368. [Opis pilota] 地球連邦軍月面特殊戦略研究所防衛隊の隊員。階級は三尉。２３歳。
   以前は月面航空隊第２５部隊に所属していたが、他の隊員が全て戦死したために隊は解散となり、研究所防衛隊に配属された。
   浅黒い肌は紫外線にさらされた影響であり、本人曰く宇宙パイロットの証。
   「人生を楽しく生きる」をモットーとした快楽主義者で、何事もポジティブに捉える不屈の精神の持ち主。
   なお、第２５部隊所属時は真面目な性格で周囲からは堅物と見られていたが、散っていった戦友達の供養として、彼等の分まで「人生を楽しく生きる」事を決め、様々な「楽しい事」に手を出している。が、少なくともナンパには不向きである。
   研究所で開発されたＥＲＳ－１００を移送するために地球へと降りた。
   ＥＲＳ－１００に「人間」というものを教えた事により、彼女の制作した機動兵器ヴァングレイに搭乗する事になり、地球を救うための旅に出る。
   そこでの経験を糧にパートナーであるナインと共に成長し、地球を救うための大きな力となった。

   EN: A member of the EFF's Lunar Special Tactical Lab Defense Force of the rank of ensign. Age 23.
   
   Formerly part of the 25th Squadron, Lunar Aviation Wing until his entire team perished in combat; this warranted the squad's disbandment and his assignment to the lab defense force. His dark-tanned skin is the result of UV-ray exposure. He claims it's the mark of a space pilot.
   
   He is a hedonist whose motto is to "enjoy life", which gives him an indomitable mindset able to see the silver lining in anything. While originally more of a serious, even inflexible person, after the death of his comrades he chose to enjoy life for their sakes as well and became focused on seeking pleasure. For all that, however, he is very poor at hitting on women.
   
   He descended to Earth accompanying the recently developed ERS-100.
   
   After teaching ERS-100 about humans, he would take command of the mobile weapon she developed, the VangRay, he then went on a journey to save the world.
   
   He learned a great deal alongside his partner Nine and was key to saving the Earth.

   EN: A member of the EFF's Lunar Special Tactical Lab Defense Force of the rank of ensign. Age 19.
   
   Having lost her parents and sister in war, she has no living family. She has no real experience in combat, and became a member of the lab defense force due to a shortage of personnel.
   
   She's only received basic training in mobile weapon piloting, but she's well regarded for her many aptitudes, particularly in shooting.
   
   Her good cheer and enthusiasm brightens the hearts of everyone around and keeps up the energy of the research staff.
   
   Because of the many tasks she undertakes around the lab outside of her duties, and by special request of the research staff, she isn't required to be in uniform.
   
   After teaching ERS-100 about humans, she would take command of the mobile weapon the system developed, the VangRay, she then went on a journey to save the world.
   
   She learned a great deal alongside her partner Nine and was key to saving the Earth.
   PL:

1370. [Opis pilota] ヴァングレイの制御ＯＳであるシステム９９が組み込まれたアンドロイド。
   ソウジやチトセとはぐれた際、情報の収集や円滑なコミュニケーションのために人間の姿の方が都合がいいと判断し、女性の人格に合わせ、システム９９自らが少女型ボディを造り上げた。
   その正体は地球再生システム「ＥＲＳ－１００」であり、人間に憧れ、人間を学ぶために、その姿を真似ている。なお、スチームパンク風の衣装は、機械と人間の間にある自分のアイデンティティを表現している。
   システム９９の時よりも感情が豊かになったが、基本的には常に冷静で、周囲に対してシニカルな態度を取る。これは極度にシャイな性格を隠すための一種の強がりと思春期の少女に見られる、いわゆる反抗期の産物である。
   超文明ガーディムの生み出した文明再建システム「ネバンリンナ」の端末であり、スレイブナンバー２０４４のコードを持つ。情報収集を目的として、調査活動を行なっていた際、トラブルからネバンリンナから独立する事になり、後に地球人に発見されてＥＲＳ－１００として調整された。
   なおネバンリンナの端末の役目は外部の情報収集であり、本来ならばネバンリンナと独立した意思を持つ事はあり得ない。しかし、トラブルによって本体の意思が遮断された事で端末内の補助ＡＩが独自の自我を持ったと推測される。
   その機能を用いて、ヴァングレイ他の機体を製造し、大切に想う人達を守り、彼等と共に戦うようになる。
   三つの世界の地球を救った後は、大切な人達と共に各地の復興のために旅に出る事を決めた。
   limit: do 1728 znaków
   EN: An android with the VangRay's operating system, System 99, installed into it.
   
   When separated from Soji and Chitose, she judged it would be better to have a human form to facilitate information gathering and ease communication. She synthesized this with a feminine personality and constructed the body of a small girl for herself.
   
   She is in fact the Earth Regeneration System ERS-100, and she chose to construct a human body out of her adoration for humanity and desire to learn about them. Her steampunk-ish attire is an attempt at expressing her own identity as someone in between machine and humanity.
   
   While more emotional than when she was System 99, she remains fundamentally level-headed and often takes a cynical attitude towards those around her. This tends to come off as a girl trying to mask her shyness with a strong front, almost like a rebellious phase.
   
   Her true nature is a "terminal" of the Gardim supercivilization's creation, the Civilization Reconstruction System Nevanlinna, and she's assigned the moniker Slave 2044. Her purpose is information gathering, but after she encountered trouble during an investigation she was disconnected from Nevanlinna and rebuilt into ERS-100 by the Earthlings who discovered her.
   
   As a rule, Nevanlinna's terminals exist solely to gather information and should not be able to possess their own individual will. It's believed that when she was disconnected from the core unit, a support AI inside the terminal activated and gained its own sense of self.
   
   Using her abilities, she built the VangRay and other machines and fought alongside the people she cared for to keep them safe. After saving the three Earths, she set out on a journey across the worlds to help rebuild.
   PL:

1371. [Opis pilota] 魔従教団でドグマを修めた術士。１８歳。
   「菫青石（きんせいせき）の術士」の二つ名を持つ。
   アル・ワースとは別世界で生まれた異界人であり、本名は「葵伊織」。
   勝ち気かつ生真面目で強い正義感を持っている。
   幼き頃より修練を積んできたドグマに自信と誇りを持ち、その力で何が出来るかを模索している。
   魔従教団を脱走後、魔法生物ホープスと共にオート・ウォーロック、ゼルガードを駆り、旅をしていた。
   教主となる素質を持った人間を集めていた魔従教団によって招喚された異界人であり、アル・ワースで過ごした時間は約一年程度であるが、精神制御下にあった時には幼き頃から教団で修練していたと思い込まされていた。
   元の世界では、感情をはっきりと示す事が出来ない自分を変えたいと願っており、その「破壊の意思」をエンデに見込まれる事となった。
   様々な人々と出会う中、自らのドグマですべき事を学んで魔力を高め、独自のドグマを完成させていった。
   また、当初は険悪であったホープスとの関係も徐々に変化していき、相棒と言うべき存在となっていった。
   アル・ワースを救った後も完全に世界が平穏を取り戻すまで、アル・ワースに留まる事を決めた。
   limit: do 1728 znaków
   EN: A sorcerer with the Keepers of Order, trained in Dogma. Age 18.
   
   Also known as the Iolite Sorcerer.
   
   He is an otherworlder born from a world beyond Al-Warth, originally called Iori Aoi.
   
   Fierce of spirit with a strong sense of justice. He has trained extensively in Dogma since his youth and is confident in his ability, but now is searching for something he can do to contribute with this ability.
   
   After leaving the Keepers of Order, he departs on a journey aboard the Autowarlock Xelguard, together with the magical creature Spero.
   
   The Keepers of Order summoned him as part of a project to collect humans with the qualities of their Founder. In fact, he's only been in Al-Warth for around a year, but his memories have been magically falsified so he believes he's been training with the Keepers from a young age.
   
   In his original world he desired to change himself and his difficulty expressing his emotions, but this "destructive will" would catch Ende's attention.
   
   Through many encounters he slowly came to see what he could do with his Dogma abilities and strengthened his magic, ultimately creating his own unique Dogma.
   
   His relationship with Spero also slowly improved from its original hostility, and he eventually came to regard Spero as a partner.
   
   After saving Al-Warth, he chose to remain there to help ensure a thorough, lasting peace returned.
   PL:

   Also known as the Iolite Sorcerer.
   
   He is an otherworlder born from a world beyond Al-Warth, originally called Iori Aoi.
   
   Although he is a serious, just person, he can be weak-willed at times.
   
   The Keepers of Order summoned him as part of a project to collect humans with the qualities of their Founder. In fact, he's only been in Al-Warth for around a year, but his memories have been magically falsified so he believes he's been training with the Keepers from a young age.
   
   In his original world he was a classmate of Amari who had feelings for her, but his weak-willed nature made it hard for him to approach her in any real way.
   
   After he regained his self thanks to Amari, he joined her in battle aboard the Xelguard, though he maintained a poor relationship with his love rival(?), Spero.
   
   After saving Al-Warth, he chose to join Amari in remaining there to help ensure a thorough, lasting peace returned.

   EN: A sorcerer with the Keepers of Order, trained in Dogma. Age 18.

   In her old world, she desired to cast aside the shy, reclusive parts of her, but this "destructive will" would catch Ende's attention.
   
   Through many encounters she slowly came to see what she could do with her Dogma abilities and strengthened her magic, ultimately creating her own unique Dogma.
   
   The strength wrapped inside her gentle heart also utterly charmed Spero.
   
   After saving Al-Warth, she chose to remain there to help ensure a thorough, lasting peace returned.

   EN: A sorcerer with the Keepers of Order, trained in Dogma. Age 18.
   
   Also known as the Aquamarine Sorcerer.
   
   She is an otherworlder born from a world beyond Al-Warth, and her real name is Amari Amano.
   
   When in the Order she seemed to be very serious and inflexible, but normally she's very polite and gentle.
   
   The Keepers of Order summoned her as part of a project to collect humans with the qualities of their Founder. In fact, she's only been in Al-Warth for around a year, but her memories have been magically falsified so she believes she's been training with the Keepers from a young age.
   
   In her original world, she was a classmate of Iori who had feelings for him that she never had the courage to act upon.

1375. [Opis pilota] ドグマで創られた人工生命体。
   偏屈かつ慇懃無礼で、自分より下と見た相手に対しては口調は丁寧だが、その端々で見下したような言動を取る。
   幅広い知識を持ち、その知識を広げるために旅をしている。
   オドを増幅する力を持っており、エンデの加護から外れつつあったゼルガードがドグマを使えたのは、ホープスの力による所が大きい。
   その正体は魔獣エンデと同じく高次元生物の幼生体であり、エンデが自らの生命のスペアとするために別の世界より呼び寄せたが、その運命から逃れるために教団から脱走した。
   エンデと同様に人間の感情を食するが、様々な出会いにより、エンデとは逆に人間の喜びや愛や希望を好むようになった。
   limit: do 1321 znaków
   EN: An artificial life form created with Dogma.
   
   He veils his distaste for those he finds to be "lesser" with a polite effect, but it's still fairly obvious when he's looking down on someone.
   
   His studies have been broad and deep, and sets out on a journey to further expand his knowledge. He has the power to enhance Od, which contributed significantly to allowing the Xelguard to use Dogma even after Ende stripped his boon away.
   
   He is in fact an infantile higher-dimensional creature akin to Ende the Devourer, and though Ende summoned him to use as a spare life for himself, Spero escaped from the Keepers to avoid that fate.
   
   Although he feeds on human emotions in the same way Ende does, his encounters with countless people ultimately send him down a path that leads him to become Ende's opposite, favoring human emotions such as hope, love, and joy.
   PL:

1376. [Opis pilota] 株式会社ＶＴＸユニオンの特務三課の主任。２７歳。
   三課では試作機「ティラネード」のメインパイロットを務める。
   サラリーマンである事に誇りを持ち、業務の遂行に生命を懸けているが、それ以外の事には無頓着であり、周囲からは奇人と見られている。
   その無頼な言動は、いい言い方をすれば「ワイルド」、悪い言い方をすれば「傍若無人」であり、「ネクタイを締めた野生の獣」として陰では「野獣ネクタイ」と呼ばれている。
   ＶＴＸ入社前はエアレースのプライベーターであり、一匹狼的な性格は、その頃に由来する。入社後数年は、その性格が災いして社内で孤立気味であったが、アマサキ係長（当時）の指導によって皆で力を合わせるサラリーマンの生き方に感銘を受け、以降は会社の一員である事に強い誇りを持つようになる。
   しかし、それ故にプライベートな時間が軽視されており、周囲の人間には呆れと心配が混じった感情を向けられている。
   黎明戦争後は「ＶＴＸユニオンの社長になる」という新たな目標を掲げ、特務三課を引っ張っていくようになった。
   limit: do 1321 znaków
   EN: Chief of the VTX Union Co.'s Special Section 3. Age 27.
   
   He serves as Section 3's main pilot for the experimental Tyranado.
   
   To him, being a salaryman is something to be proud of; he's dedicated to his job, but otherwise relatively indifferent to most other things, which serves to give others the impression that he's a bit strange.
   
   His crude attitude can be said to be free and unbound...in a positive light. In a more negative light, one could say he has no consideration for others at all. This leads to some calling him a "beast with a necktie", or simply "the necktie beast", behind his back.
   
   Prior to joining VTX, he was an air-race privateer, and his solo-flying attitude is a leftover from his speed demon days. A few years after joining VTX, his personality caused him trouble and threatened to isolate him, but manager Amasaki's guidance helped him find value in the life of a salaryman, of being someone who works together with the people around him, and ever since he has been fiercely proud of being a member of the company.
   
   This had the side effect of making him disregard his personal time, which earns confusion and concern from the people around him.
   
   Following the Daybreak War, he found a new goal for himself—to become president of VTX Union Co., a goal which drives him to lead Special Section 3.
   PL:

1378. [Opis pilota] 株式会社ＶＴＸユニオンの特務三課の主任。２７歳。
   三課では試作機「ティラネード」のメインパイロットを務める。
   可憐なルックスを持ちながら、「ずぼら、大雑把、テキトー」な性格により、見た目とのギャップで「堕天使」の異名を持つ。
   その天才的な操縦技術が社内でも注目されているが、本人は至ってマイペースであり、「給料とボーナスのために働く」と公言している。
   そういった自由な生き方と無意識に繰り出される男前な言動によって、社内に多数の女性ファンを持ち、あだ名の「堕天使」は「天使を堕とす者」の意味も持つ。
   入社時の配属希望先は「食品部門でお菓子の企画」であったが、適性検査によって高い操縦能力を持つ事が判明し、その方面の配属となった過去を持つ。
   なお、照れ屋な所があり、その自由奔放な言動は照れ隠しである事が多い。主任に任命された後も柄ではないとして部下達には「センパイ」と呼ばせていたが、それは力不足の自分に対するためらいのためでもあった。
   黎明戦争後は、周囲から「いつかはＶＴＸユニオンの社長に！」と担ぎ上げられ、特務三課を引っ張っていくようになった。
   limit: do 1387 znaków
   EN: Chief of the VTX Union Co.'s Special Section 3. Age 27.
   
   She serves as Section 3's main pilot for the experimental Tyranado.
   
   Contrary to her neat, dignified appearance, she is in fact a very careless slob, and the cavernous gap between these two sides of her has earned her the nickname "Fallen Angel".
   
   While her genius-level piloting skills have earned her a great deal of attention within VTX, she nonetheless does things as she alone sees fit, stating simply she "works solely for the salary and bonuses".
   
   Her open and frank ways and unconscious masculine-esque attitude have earned her many fans amongst the women of the company, to the point that her nickname "Fallen Angel" is taken to mean that she's the one who makes angels fall.
   
   While she originally applied to join the foodstuffs division as part of the sweets design team, testing discovered her piloting abilities and so she was ultimately assigned to her current posting.
   
   She is easily embarrassed, and no small portion of her freewheeling attitude helps to disguise it.
   
   While she asked her staff to refer to her casually after she became chief, in truth this was not just because it didn't suit her, but also because of her own feelings of impostor syndrome.
   
   After the Daybreak War, people started insisting she will be the VTX Union president someday, and she is rising to the role of leader for Special Section 3.
   PL:

1380. [Opis pilota] 株式会社ＶＴＸユニオンの特務三課に配属された新入社員。２０歳。
   三課では試作機「ティラネード」のコ・パイロットを務める。
   地球連邦軍の軍事大学を飛び級で卒業した才媛であり、ＶＴＸユニオンでは戦術研究課に配属される予定であったが、ゴードウィン社長の薦めによって特務三課の所属となった。
   性格は真面目であるが、融通の利かない所があり、一度悩み始めるととことんまで考え込んでしまうクセがある。
   惑星ダーブネス出身であり、クォーク・ドライブ試作機と共に３年前に地球へとたどり着く。その後、ゴードウィン社長の保護を受け、アマサキ家の養女となった。
   本来の髪の色は青色であるが、ナノマシンによるカラーリングで地球人風の髪色にしている。これは異星人である事を隠すと同時に、地球人のファッションスタイルを好むラミィの趣味のためである。
   養父である特務三課のヒロスケ・アマサキ課長を実父同様に慕っており、完全に地球での生活に適応し、それを楽しんでいる。

   EN: A new employee assigned to VTX Union Co.'s Special Section 3. Age 20.
   
   She serves as the copilot of Section 3's experimental Tyranado.
   
   As a rare talent who graduated early from the EFF's military college, she was a shoo-in for VTX Union's tactical section, but was instead assigned to Section 3 at President Goldwin's recommendation.
   
   While a serious, devoted person, she is worryingly inflexible and often vulnerable to becoming trapped in the maze of her own cares and fears once something starts eating at her.
   
   Originally from Planet Darbness, she arrived on Earth three years ago alongside the test version of the Quark Drive. After arriving, she was taken in by President Goldwin, and was then adopted by the Amasaki family.
   
   Her hair is actually naturally blue, and is colored to look similar to Earthlings through the use of nanomachines. Naturally, this serves to disguise her extraterrestrial origins, but it also reflects Rami's fashion choices as she appreciates this aspect of Earthling culture.
   
   She cares for her adopted father Hirosuke Amasaki as though he is her real father and has adapted to life on Earth happily.

1381. [Opis pilota] 株式会社ＶＴＸユニオンの特務三課の課長。５２歳。
   プロジェクトＴＮＤの責任者であり、統合戦術支援機「キャリアクス」の操縦を担当している。
   腰の低い温和な人物で、普段の業務は部下それぞれの自主性に任せている。
   その育成能力には定評があり、何人もの問題社員を一人前に育て上げた実績を持っている。
   ＶＴＸユニオン入社前は軍人であり、その頃からの知り合いであるダイマ・ゴードウィン社長とはプライベートでも付き合いがある。
   軍人時代はダイマ・ゴードウィンの上官であり、強い正義感を持ちながらも独善的な彼に他人と協調して物事を成し遂げる事の大切さ…「サラリーマンの意味」を学ばせたいと考え、共にＶＴＸに入社した過去を持つ。
   妻との間に子供が出来なかったため、養女であるラミィを実の娘のように可愛がっているが、彼女が結婚する日の事を思ってしんみりしてしまう事もある。

   EN: Section manager of VTX Union Co.'s Special Section 3. Age 52.
   
   He is Project TND's chief manager, as well as the pilot of the tactical support unit Carriax.
   
   A humble, peaceful man, he is generally happy to let his staff manage themselves.
   
   He's well respected for his ability to drive the growth of people under him, and many of the company's problem employees have been molded into respectable team members in his hands.
   
   Prior to joining VTX, he was a military man, and he's been a personal acquaintance of President Dyma Goldwin since his military days.
   
   In the military, he was Dyma Goldwin's superior, and while he was impressed with Goldwin's strong sense of justice, he wanted to teach Goldwin to overcome the self-righteous mindset that kept him from working with others.
   
   To this end, they joined VTX together so he could teach Goldwin the spirit of a salaryman.
   
   Despite Hirosuke and his wife being unable to conceive a child, he loves his adopted daughter Rami as dearly as if she were their own. He gets very depressed when thinking of the day she goes off to get married.

   EN: Staff member of VTX Union Co.'s Special Section 3. Age 24.
   
   Formerly part of Technology Section 1, in charge of developing weaponry, she is now responsible for the management of the Tyranado and its armaments.
   
   She is also an operator aboard the tactical support unit Carriax, managing the Tyranado's weapons externally.
   
   She normally projects an air of being collected and logical, but in reality is short-tempered and very single-minded to the extent she's sometimes referred to as Meltdown Meryl (outside of earshot).
   
   As a result, despite her skills she was an endless source of trouble to the people she had to work with, earning her a label as a major, unquestioned "problem employee".
   
   She herself feels ashamed at how she acts and wants to change, and she gets a chance through her encounters with her new comrades in Section 3, helping her slowly soften her edges.
   
   She would deny it openly but she loves anything cute, including animals, trinkets, and the like.

   EN: Staff member of VTX Union Co.'s Special Section 3. Age 24.
   
   Formerly a part of the general affairs department, she is now in charge of handling similar duties for Special Section 3 specifically. She also serves as an operator aboard the full tactical support unit Carriax, using her information management skills to handle communications and sensors.
   
   Her charming appearance and gentle front, combined with her meticulous and exacting work attitude, has left in her wake a trail of male employees who "misread" the situation.
   
   While that makes her an unfortunate source of trouble (of sorts), she fends such accusations (and woebegotten suitors) off easily with a few pointed jabs of the utterly merciless scathing comments that earned her the nickname "Miss Straight".
   
   She herself isn't entirely proud of how quickly the thorns can come out and would like to change, and she gets a chance through her encounters with her new comrades in Section 3, softening the edges of her tongue. Somewhat.
   
   Still, her colleague Meryl finds her fearsome as she catches the occasional glimpse of her darker side.

1384. [Opis pilota] 超文明ガーディムが開発した文明再建システム。
   自律型のＡＩであり、女性の人格を有している。
   その性格形成には、端末であるナンバー２０４４の集めたデータが大きな影響を与えており、強い責任感と覚悟、使命遂行の障害に対する不屈の闘志を持っている。
   内部には超文明ガーディムを構成する、あらゆるデータが保存されており、崩壊寸前であったガーディム本星から少数のガーディム人と共に銀河系へと逃げ延びた。
   システムを起動させた場合、周囲の環境をガーディム人にとって最適なものに改造し、周辺の資源を使用して文明を再建するというプロセスを取る。
   使命遂行の障害を排除し、ガーディム文明を守るための戦闘機能も有しており、その手段として自らの肉体となる機動兵器アーケイディアを造り上げた。
   スレイブナンバー２０４４ことナインの集めたデータを取り込む事で、人間を超えた存在を自称したが、自らが理解できなかった「愛」という感情への恐れから自滅する。
   しかし、最終的には自らの非を悟り、自身の使命をナインに託して散っていった。

   EN: The civilization reconstruction system created by the Supercivilization Gardim.
   
   She is a fully sapient AI with a female personality. Much of her personality is based on data received from terminal number 2044, giving her a strong will and sense of responsibility, as well as an unflagging hostility to anything that would obstruct her mission.
   
   She carries inside her a complete dataset of every element of Gardim, whose people left their home planet with her in tow just prior to that world's destruction.
   
   When activated, the system's purpose is to transform the local environment into one suitable for the people of Gardim, collect available resources and use them to rebuild Gardim society.
   
   In order to remove potential obstacles, and also to defend the legacy of Gardim, she is equipped with combat capability and constructed the mobile weapon Arcadia as a body to use in combat.
   
   While she believes the data provided by Nine, a.k.a. Slave 2044, has made her into a superior being to humans, it is her fear of "love", a concept she failed to understand, that spelled her defeat.
   
   Even so, in her last moments she accepted her own wrongdoing and entrusted her mission to Nine before meeting her end.
   PL:

1385. [Opis pilota] アル・ワースの創造主。
   人知を越えた超絶的な力を持つ高次元生物であり、その意思の力で認識宇宙内にアル・ワースを創り上げた。
   その目的は、そこに人間を繁殖させ、そこで生まれる感情を食する事にあり、人間の悲しみや苦しみや絶望を好む。それらを生み出すために魔従教団を組織し、アル・ワースの戦乱を望むままにコントロールしてきた。
   その食事は生きていくために必須というわけではなく、あくまで自らの快楽のためのものであり、人間の言葉で表現すれば、残酷で無慈悲なエゴイスト。
   既に３０００年以上の時を生きており、いつかは寿命が尽きると考え、自らのスペアとなる「精神・肉体・生命」を求めている。
   その死後もアル・ワースは、そこに住む人々の願いによって存在し続けた。

   EN: The creator of Al-Warth. He is a higher dimensional creature possessed with unbelievable power who manifested Al-Warth into observable reality solely through the power of his will.
   
   His goal was to have humanity live there, which would allow him to feast on their emotions, with a specific taste for despair, sadness, pain, and other such feelings.
   
   To ensure this came to pass, he also founded the Keepers of Order and through them manipulated the tides of war to his will.
   
   Despite the metaphor of food being used when describing him, he does not consume emotions to survive as such, and humans would describe him as a cruel, merciless egoist.
   
   While he has lived for over three thousand years, he is aware that he will die, and seeks a spare "mind, body, and life" to ensure his continuity.
   
   Following his death, Al-Warth remains in existence, now instead sustained by the wishes of its inhabitants.
   PL:

1386. [Opis pilota] 株式会社ＶＴＸユニオンの元社長。４８歳。
   豪腕で知られる人物であり、３８歳という若さで世界的大企業であるＶＴＸユニオンの社長に就任し、そこから１０年間、世界の経済界をリードしてきた存在。
   ワンマン経営で内外に敵も多いが、その器の大きさに心酔し、命を投げ出す覚悟で付き従う人間も多い。
   強い意志を持ち、一度決めた事は曲げないが、経営に対しては柔軟な思考でＶＴＸを幅広い分野へと進出させた。
   入社前は軍人であり、スーパーロボット「ガイアーン」を駆り、人類の敵と戦ってきた英雄であった。
   しかし、自らの死を偽り、かねてから画策していた「銀河統一計画」を実行に移す。ＶＴＸユニオンの資産を流用して造り上げた「機動要塞ＶＴＸ」と絶対決戦兵器「ダイガイアン」を駆り、新たなる地球の守護者を自称した。
   「銀河統一計画」を心の底から人類のためになると信じており、その遂行こそが自らの使命であり、正義であると考えていた。
   ＶＴＸユニオンの社長業を務めながら、ダイガイアンのパイロットとして再び戦場に立つ日のためにトレーニングに明け暮れ、軍人時代以上の戦闘力を持つ。
   敗北の後は自分の非を認めて投降し、その計画は終焉を迎えた。

   EN: The former president of VTX Union Co. Age 48.
   
   Known to be incredibly capable, he became president of VTX Union at the relatively young age of 38 and has held the reins of world economics for over ten years.
   
   He handles everything himself at the management level and has many enemies inside and outside the company, but his capabilities also inspire a fervor in others that make them follow him even at the cost of their lives.
   
   While generally the sort to never go back on a decision, his flexible thinking as a manager has enabled VTX to develop across countless fields of business.
   
   Prior to joining the company, he was a military man, a hero who fought the enemies of humanity aboard the super robot Gaian. Later, he faked his own death and set out to execute what he referred to as the Galactic Unification Plan.
   
   He used the resources of VTX to construct the Mobile Fortress VTX, as well as the "ultimate victory weapon" Dygaian, proclaiming himself Earth's new guardian.
   
   He sincerely and truly believes the Galactic Unification Plan is in humanity's best interest and feels it his duty to see this "moral good" completed.
   
   While serving as president of the company, he also trained to ensure he would be able to stand on the battlefield once more aboard Dygaian, making him even more capable than in his military days.
   
   Following his defeat, he surrendered, signaling the end of his plan.
   PL:

1387. [Opis pilota] クエスターズの実行部隊隊長。
   第１２銀河の戦闘種族ヴァウルーガの民であり、仲間達と共にクエスターズにスカウトされたという経歴を持つ。
   クエスターズ指導者の代理人として、地球文明の調査や実験を主任務とする。
   「先生」によって、審判の後に地球の王になる事を指示されており、その証として与えられる機体・ヴァリンの最上位機であるグラヴァリンを駆る。
   しかし、地球文明との接触の中、真理の探究者の一員らしく様々なものに興味を持った結果、傲慢さは徐々に消えていき、新鮮な体験の連続に驚く純粋さが目立つようになる。さらには自らと「先生」の関係にまで疑問を持つに至る。
   その正体は、他のクエスターズの構成員と同じく「先生」のクローンであり、記憶も思考も「先生」に用意されたものであった。
   「先生」の死後は「次元の番人」を自称し、知的好奇心を満たすために様々な並行世界を旅している。

   Paradoxically, in his encounters with the civilization of Earth, he acted upon his curiosity just as he thought a seeker of truth ought to, and the stream of new experiences gradually peeled back his egotism to reveal a surprising innocence beneath.    EN: His real name is Isaac Godunov, creator and leader of the Cosmo Rangers J9.
   
   With his brilliant analytical and strategic mind, he serves as the brains of J9, handing out orders to the other members, all of whom trust him completely.
   
   He was born to an aristocratic northern European family, but lost his father to a political power struggle at an early age. His life since then is shrouded in mystery, but at some point he began to operate a troubleshooting organization from the J9 base, located in the asteroid belt's West Zone, J Ward 9th District.
   
   Though he has a cold exterior, beneath it is a quiet warrior fighting for justice, whose heart burns with hatred of authority and evil.
   
   For the most part, he will accept any job as long as the price is right, though he does maintain a policy of rejecting requests from evildoers or anyone else he doesn't personally like.
   
   He is a master of martial arts and lashes out ruthlessly at his evil foes with his favorite electric whip.
   
   His cold exterior belies a warm sense of humor and a humanitarian side demonstrated when he took in Sin and Mei despite having no relation to them.
   
   His biggest dislikes are lending money and eating green peppers, and his greatest weakness is his inability to hold his liquor.

   EN: The number one negotiator in Paradigm City, the city that has lost its memory.
   
   Twenty-five years old. Dastun, the chief of the Military Police, is his former commanding officer.
   
   His sense of humor and wit give him a startling eloquence that often proves key in negotiation and ensures he always exceeds his clients expectations.
   
   He is generally chivalrous and a gentleman, but he can be swayed by his emotions and is not too shy about using force against people who try to stonewall him, act disrespectfully, or break the rules. Despite this, he makes a point of never carrying a gun no matter how dangerous the situation he faces is.
   
   While he tends to be extremely stubborn in his decision-making, he also can be very caring, such as when he changed his rules to let Dorothy keep a cat.
   
   He has the Memories needed to pilot Big O, and uses Big O to face off against oversized threats. However, he does so not out of any belief in himself as a hero but instead simply as justified punishment to those who act without sincerity or break the rules of decent human behavior.
   
   Incidentally, Roger himself has no idea why he has Memories regarding the piloting of
   Big O.

   While he prefers a languid, peaceful rise from bed, these days he is often woken up in the morning by Dorothy's intense piano performances.

   EN: The Magnetic Robot developed by Dr. Shiba to counter the Jamatai Kingdom.
   
   Kotetsu Jeeg is formed when the Jeeg Parts, launched from the Big Shooter, combine with the Jeeg Head (which Hiroshi Shiba transforms into).
   Powered by magnetic current energy, it boasts an impressive output of 750,000 horsepower.
   
   In addition to close-quarters combat techniques like the Dynamite Kick and Jeeg Breaker, it is equipped with ranged weapons such as the Knuckle Bomber and Spin Storm. Its most distinctive feature, however, is the Magnet Power, which allows Jeeg to easily swap parts during battle.
   
   Jeeg initially has access to the Mach Drill and the Jeeg Bazooka, but his arsenal is expanded throughout the story to include additional equipment such as the Jeeg Buckler, the Sky Parts, the Earth Parts, and the Marine Parts, enabling Hiroshi to adapt to any combat situation.
   PL:

1422. [Opis pilota (DLC2)] 緑ヶ浜小学校の５年生。
   性格はお調子者だが、正義感が強い。

   軍の高官である父・光一郎とアナウンサーの母・美鈴が二人共、不在がちであるため家では一人でいる事が多く、料理が得意。
   良くも悪くも純粋であり、思った事をすぐに口にしたり、考え無しに行動したりして自らピンチを招く事も多いが、その真っ直ぐさにより勇者達と協力して、地球を守るために戦っていく。

   EN: A fifth grader at Midorigahama Elementary.
   
   He has a playful and mischievous personality but also a strong sense of justice.
   
   Entrusted with the Aurin by what might be called the will of the Earth itself, he becomes the commander of the Brave Fighters.
   
   Since both his father, Koichiro, a high-ranking military officer, and his mother, Misuzu, a news anchor, are frequently away from home, he often spends time alone; as a result, he has become surprisingly skilled at cooking.
   
   Seiji is a pure-hearted boy, in both good and bad senses—he speaks his mind without hesitation and often acts impulsively, occasionally landing himself in trouble.
   However, it is thanks to his unwavering sincerity that he is able to cooperate with the Brave Fighters and continue fighting to protect the Earth.

1433. [Opis pilota (DLC3)] ソロ星科学庁長官ユウキ・ロウル博士の息子で、Ａメカ及びイデオンのパイロット。１６歳。
   ソロ星がバッフ・クランに襲撃を受けた際、身を守るためにイデオンのＡメカに乗り込み、なし崩し的にイデオンのパイロットとなる。
   身勝手な大人達や異星人バッフ・クランに対する怒り、生への執着心で当初は戦い抜いたが、無限エネルギー・イデの危険性が明確になるにつれ、戦いに対する考え方が変わっていく。
   特技はナイフ投げで、飛行中の超小型偵察機を落とすほどの腕前である。また、機械いじりも得意で、イデオンの電子機器の整備も手伝っていた。
   皮肉屋で斜に構えた性格だったが、ブラジラー基地で出会ったカミューラ・ランバン、植民星キャラルで出会ったキッチ・キッチンらの死をはじめ、バッフ・クランからの逃避行の中での様々な出来事がコスモを大人にさせていった。
   開拓者として育ったためか、過酷な環境下でも諦めることなく戦い続ける強さを持ち、バッフ・クランとの最終決戦における絶望的な状況でも、最後まで生き抜こうとする姿勢をやめなかった。
   limit: do 1332 znaków
   EN: The 16-year-old son of Dr. Rawl Yuki, Director of the Science Agency on Planet Solo. He is the pilot of Ideon's A Mecha and of Ideon itself.
   
   When Planet Solo was attacked by the Buff Clan, he boarded the A Mecha to survive; in doing so, he was unwillingly thrust into the role of Ideon's main pilot.
   
   At first, he fought out of sheer anger—toward both the selfish adults around him and the alien Buff Clan—as well as out of a stubborn refusal to die. But as the dangers of the Ide's infinite energy became increasingly clear, his perspective on war began to change.
   
   He is highly skilled at knife-throwing, capable of hitting small reconnaissance drones mid-flight. He also has a knack for machinery, and often helped with the maintenance of Ideon's electronics.
   
   Though initially cynical and standoffish, the deaths of Camulla Lanban, the commander at the Brazillier base, and Kittie Kitten, whom he met on the colony planet Carale, as well as the many hardships he endured during his group's desperate flight from the Buff Clan forced Cosmo to mature.
   
   Perhaps due to his upbringing as a settler, he possesses remarkable resilience, always willing to fight on no matter how harsh the conditions are. Even in the face of the hopeless final battle against the Buff Clan, he never abandoned his will to survive 'till the bitter end.
   PL:

   最初は足手まといでしかなかったが、コスモのサポートに欠かせないパイロットに成長し、熱くなりすぎるコスモを抑える役目に回ることも度々あった。ギジェを欠き、カーシャが降りた後はＢメカのメインパイロットとなり、完全に一人前の戦士となっていた。

   EN: One of the children from Planet Solo and Cosmo's sidekick. He is 10 years old.

   He lost both parents during the Buff Clan attack. Although he was initially a non-combatant, his deep hatred for the Buff Clan and childlike admiration for heroes drove him to volunteer as the subpilot of Ideon's A Mecha.
   
   At first, he was little more than a burden, but over time, he grew into Cosmo's most reliable assistant, often stepping in to temper his hot-headedness.

   She received a specialized education for gifted children and holds a rocket mechanic's license.
   Having grown up away from her parents on A-92, a colony planet with only summer and winter seasons, she developed a strong sense of self-reliance. As a result, she has a tendency to act impulsively and independently, often antagonizing those around her.

   常にポジティブで芯の強さを見せるが、同時にあまり深く考えずに状況を受け入れる淡泊な面や心の弱さも併せ持つ。
   リボンがトレードマークで、周囲の人間からそれにちなんだあだ名で呼ばれる事が多い。
   戦いの中、インベルに心がある事を知り、強い信頼関係を築いていき、それは恋愛感情にまで昇華された。

   EN: The master of the iDOL Imber.
   
   She applied to an idol audition which turned out to be one for iDOL pilots. Now she's an idolmaster under the international agency Mondenkind, which helps rebuilding efforts in the wake of worldwide disaster.
   
   Though she is normally positive and steadfast, she also has a tendency to be a bit too carefree with accepting whatever situation is presented to her, and cracks of weakness sometimes manifest in her heart. Her hair ribbon is her trademark, and those around her often give her nicknames based on it. After she discovered Imber had a heart of its own, she developed a deep trust in her iDOL which eventually blossomed into love.

1438. [Opis pilota (DLC3)] 南十字島へやってきた少年。

   EN: A boy who arrived on Southern Cross Island.
   
   He enrolls as a first-year student at Southern Cross High School and joins the Midnight Flight drama club.
   
   His relentless positivity and energy makes him instantly popular in school.
   
   He is the Star Driver of the Cybody Tauburn and a Ginga Bishonen who has reached Phase 3, allowing him to pilot Tauburn directly.
   
   Wako was the first to find him washed up on the island's shores. He develops a rivalry with Sugata over her favor, as well as a deep friendship.
   
   He opposes the Kiraboshi Order of Cross's plans to take control of the Cybodies' powers. This leads to a final battle with the king Cybody King Samekh, taken over by the Order's Head (in reality his father Tokio) for his personal ambitions. In his victory, he frees Sugata and Wako from their fates.
   
   Afterwards, he leaves Southern Cross Island with Sugata and Wako, hunting down the other Cybodies now released from zero-time.

   EN: The heir of the Shindo family, a prominent Southern Cross Island dynasty.
   
   He is a first-year student at Southern Cross High School like Takuto, and a member of the Midnight Flight drama club.
   
   Not only is he incredibly handsome, he has an intellectual and composed bearing that makes a sharp contrast to Takuto, earning them both a lot of attention from female students.
   
   He has known Wako from childhood and is also in an arranged betrothal. Though he doesn't show it, he is fiercely dedicated to her safety.
   
   He is also a student of traditional Japanese combat arts and is Takuto's instructor as well as his rival for Wako's favor.
   
   He is the Star Driver of King Samekh, the king Cybody. He had a plan to sacrifice himself to seal away King Samekh's all-consuming libido-draining power, but the massive Cybody was hijacked by Head.
   
   Takuto defeated Samekh, freeing him from his chains, and he was able to chart his own path at last.
