   EN: A girl from Southern Cross Island.
   
   When Takuto tried to swim to the island and washed up on the beach, she is the one who found him, and they only grew closer afterwards.
   
   Like Takuto, she's a first-year student at Southern Cross High School and a member of the Midnight Flight drama club.
   
   She's upbeat, energetic, and prone to flights of fancy.
   
   She is the Southern Maiden, one of the Shrine Maidens of the Four Directions tasked with keeping the Cybodies sealed. This keeps her on the island despite her dreams to be a singer.
   
   She is a childhood acquaintance and the arranged fiancée of Sugata, but her feelings for Takuto are just as strong.
   
   During the battle with King Samekh, she apprivoised with her Cybody Wawna, but it was destroyed, her seal was broken, and zero-time was erased.
   
   Takuto's victory over Samekh meant she and Sugata were freed from their fates and able to decide their own lives.

   データストームの中でしか生存できないエリクトの居場所を作るため、プロスペラはクワイエット・ゼロを建造し、エリクトはそれを止めようとするスレッタたちの前に立ち塞がった。

   EN: Prospera's daughter.
   
   During the Vanadis Incident twenty-one years ago, she fled to Mercury with her mother. However, she was unable to withstand the environment in space, prompting Prospera to use GUND technology to transfer her biometric code into the Gundam Aerial.
   
   Since then, Ericht has been the very body of the Gundam Aerial, protecting the pilot, Suletta, from data storms.
   
   Prospera constructed Quiet Zero to create a place for Ericht to live, since she could only live in data storms. When Suletta and the others tried to stop them, Ericht stood in their way.

1474. [Opis pilota (DLC4)] ３０００年後の未来世界で人類と戦いを繰り広げる謎の存在「ウツロ」が、その時代の特戦隠密「朧」の頭領である月ノ輪プサイに擬態したもの。
   人間という存在に興味を持ち、人類の歴史の大きな転換期を観察に来た。
   成長や繁殖、感情や生命といったものを持たない「ウツロ」は生物とは異なる何かであり、「概念」が形を成したものである。そのため物理的なダメージを受けても三次元世界に置かれた姿がいくらかの影響を受けるだけであり、その根源が消滅する事はない。「ウツロ」を消滅させるためには、高次元にある真の存在を直接攻撃するか、三次元世界上の概念を崩壊させる必要がある。
   人間から心や感情を学んだ事により、その概念は失われ、代わりに生命を得る。
   limit: do 866 znaków
   EN: The mysterious being known as the Void who fights against humankind in the future world 3000 years from now, in its mimicry of Psi Tsukinowa, the head of the Special Stealth Forces Haze in that era.
   
   Its interest in the beings known as humans prompted it to observe a major turning point in mankind's history.
   
   The Void is not a living creature—it cannot grow or reproduce, and it has no emotions or life. Rather, it is a concept made manifest. As such, attacking it physically only has a minor effect on the part of it that exists within the three-dimensional world; such attempts will never be able to destroy the Void. In order to do so, one needs to directly attack its true form in a higher dimension or destroy its concept in the three-dimensional world.
   
   By learning from humans about hearts and emotions, its concept was lost, and it gained life in exchange.
   PL:

   虚獣マグダと融合した事で「ウツロ」に触れ、その精神は異常をきたして自我は崩壊している。残されているのは醜いエゴと自身の最高傑作であるエチカへの執着だけであり、ただひたすらにエチカを求め続ける。

1476. [Opis pilota (DLC4)] スコート・ラボ所属の次元獣バスター。
   破界事変では愛機ブラスタと共に自由と平和を求めて戦い、多額の借金の返済にも成功した。
   ニヒルな無頼漢を気取るが、そのポーズが長続きしない二枚目半。様々な特技を持ち、軍隊格闘術システマを駆使し、生身での戦闘でも実力を発揮する。
   女嫌いを自称し、美人は信用しないと公言する。その原因は過去に由来するが、本人は語ろうとしない。
   軍の特殊部隊「ファイヤバグ」に所属していた事を悔いており、それについて触れられる事を嫌がる。
   破界事変後はサラリーマン次元獣バスターとして、世界各地に出没する次元獣退治を生業としていたが、新たな借金を背負う事になり、戦いに身を投じていく。
   その戦いの中、新たな愛機リ・ブラスタと共に「揺れる天秤」のスフィアに目覚めていく。が、借金の宿命から逃れる事は出来なかった。
   超時空修復後も借金返済に追われるが、それなりに満足のいく日々を送る。
   limit: do 1201 znaków
   EN: A Damon Buster working for the Scott Lab.
   
   He fought for peace and freedom in his mech, the Brasta, during the Time of Ruination and repaid his hefty debt in the process.
   
   He tries to look like a pessimist and a cad, but he always ends up outing himself as a joker. He has many skills, and with his training in the military martial art Cistema, can fight on foot as well as in a robot.
   
   He claims to distrust women—especially pretty ones—because of something that happened in his past, but he refuses to elaborate.
   
   His membership in the Firebugs, a military special ops squad, is one of his biggest regrets, and he doesn't like it when the subject is brought up.
   
   After the Time of Ruination came to an end, he became a full-time Damon Buster, exterminating the Damons that continued to appear around the world. Eventually, though, he once again ended up burdened with debt and threw himself back into the fray.
   
   As he fought on in his new mech, the Li-Brasta, his Sphere—the Wavering Scales—began to awaken. In the end, he was never able to escape from his debt-ridden destiny.
   
   Debt collectors hounded him even after the dimensions were repaired, but he's fairly satisfied with his life regardless.
   PL:

1479. [Opis pilota (DLC4)] プロスペラの計画が進行する中、ミオリネに見放されたショックと、出生の秘密を知り、プロスペラとエアリアルとのつながりも断ち切れたことで失意の底にいた。しかし、戦闘で被害を受けた学園の復旧に奔走し、自分のやるべき事を見つけていく。
   自身がプロスペラの実の娘であるエリクト・サマヤの遺伝子から作られた「リプリチャイルド」であり、ガンダムの呪いを受けなかったのはエアリアルに生体コードを移されたエリクトが代わりに引き受けていたためである事を知った後、プロスペラとエリクトを止めるために行動する。
   クワイエット・ゼロ突入作戦では、データストームに苦しみながらもガンダム・キャリバーンを駆り、最後はエリクトと共に宇宙議会連合の惑星間レーザー送電システムを停止させた。
   クワイエット・ゼロの事件から３年後は、データストームによりダメージを負った体のリハビリを行ないながら、幸せに暮らしている。

   EN: As Prospera's plan progressed, she fell into a deep despair from the shock of being abandoned by Miorine, discovering the truth about her birth, and losing her connection to Prospera and the Aerial. However, while restoring the school after it was damaged in battle, she began to figure out what she needed to do.
   
   After learning that she was a repli-child created from the genes of Ericht Samaya, Prospera's real daughter, and that she hadn't suffered from the Gundam's curse because Ericht, whose biometric code had been transferred into the Aerial, had borne it in her place, she took action to stop Prospera and Ericht.
   
   During the operation to breach Quiet Zero, she piloted the Gundam Calibarn while enduring the data storm. In the end, she and Ericht stopped the Space Assembly League's interplanetary laser power transmission system.
   
   Three years after the Quiet Zero incident, she lives happily while undergoing rehabilitation for the damage her body suffered from the data storm.
   PL:

1480. [Opis pilota (DLC4)] プロスペラによるスレッタへのマインドコントロールともいえる扱いを知り、今後プロスペラがスレッタとかかわらないことを条件に次期総裁になることを決意する。
   総裁選への実績作りのために抗議デモを続ける地球側との会談に臨むが、プロスペラの計略によって大規模な戦闘が起きた事で交渉は決裂する。自責の念に駆られ自室に閉じこもっていたが、スレッタの真っ直ぐな気持ちに救われ、前に進む気概を取り戻す。
   周囲と協力してクワイエット・ゼロへ直接乗り込みその機能を停止させた後、ベネリットグループ総裁として事件の終息を図るためにグループ解散を宣言した。
   クワイエット・ゼロの事件から３年後は、自身の起こしたクイン・ハーバーでの惨劇の償いのために、自らにできることを続けている。
   limit: do 1070 znaków
   EN: Upon learning that Prospera had been manipulating Suletta in a manner bordering on mind control, she decided to run for president on the condition that Prospera would have no further involvement with Suletta.
   
   To build her record for the presidential election, she began negotiations with the Earth side, which was holding protest demonstrations. However, the talks broke down when Prospera's scheme caused widespread fighting. Driven by guilt, she shut herself away in her room. However, she was saved by Suletta's sincerity and earnestness, regaining the will to move forward.
   
   After working with her allies to board Quiet Zero and disable it, she used her position as president of the Benerit Group to dissolve the Group and bring the incident to an end.
   
   Three years after the Quiet Zero incident, she continues to do what she can to atone for the Quin Harbor tragedy she unwittingly sparked.
   PL:

1481. [Opis pilota (DLC4)] 学園を去った後は「ボブ」を名乗り、輸送船で働いていた。
   反スペーシアン組織「フォルドの夜明け」のプラント・クエタ襲撃に巻き込まれ、モビルスーツで脱出するが、テロリストに誤認された結果、父・ヴィムの操縦する機体を意図せず撃墜してしまう。
   その後、父とのつながりである会社を立て直すため、学園に帰還しＣＥＯに就任。
   ミオリネと共に地球へ降りるが、そこでシャディクが「フォルドの夜明け」とのつながりがあることを知り、自らの手でシャディクの計画を阻止する。
   クワイエット・ゼロ突入作戦にも参加し、ミオリネを狙う弟・ラウダの駆るガンダム・シュバルゼッテと交戦する。グエルの身を挺した説得に救われ、フェルシーの助けもあり兄弟ともに生還を果たす。
   クワイエット・ゼロの事件から３年後は、ジェターク社ＣＥＯとして、エランやセセリアの協力を受け、やや立場の弱さに付け込まれながらも会社の再建に尽力している。
   limit: do 1070 znaków
   EN: After leaving the school, he adopted the name "Bob" and found work on a transport ship.
   
   He was caught up in the attack on Plant Quetta by the anti-Spacian organization, the Dawn of Fold, and escaped in a Mobile Suit. However, after being mistaken for a terrorist, he unintentionally shot down the unit piloted by his father, Vim.
   
   After that, he returned to school and became CEO in order to rebuild the company that connected him to his father.
   
   He descended to Earth with Miorine. Upon learning that Shaddiq had ties to the Dawn of Fold, he intervened to stop his plan.
   
   He also participated in the operation to breach Quiet Zero and fought against the Gundam Schwarzette, which was targeting Miorine and piloted by his younger brother, Lauda. Thanks to Guel's persuasion at the risk of his own life and Felsi's help, both brothers survived.
   
   Three years after the Quiet Zero incident, he serves as the CEO of Jeturk Heavy Machinery and is devoted to rebuilding it with the help of Elan and Secelia, although they often take advantage of his relatively weak standing.
   PL:

   EN: Her real name is Elnora Samaya.
   
   After fleeing to Mercury, she transferred her beloved daughter Ericht's biometric code into a Gundam in an effort to preserve her existence after she died at a young age.
   
   She also created the repli-child Suletta as the key to raising the Permet Score and completed Quiet Zero to create a place where Ericht could live, as she could only exist within the data storm.
   
   Prospera's plan was ultimately thwarted by Suletta and her friends, and after accepting Suletta's and Ericht's wishes, she spends the rest of her life peacefully with them.

   この日を予見していた南原博士は、超電磁の力で５体のマシンが合体する巨大ロボのコン・バトラーＶを建造。日本全国から集めた葵豹馬、浪花十三、西川大作、北小介の４人に孫娘の南原ちずるを加えた「バトルチーム」を結成する。
   しかし、戦いの中で南原博士は生命を落とし、後は博士の友人であった四ッ谷博士に託される。
   キャンベル星人の大将軍ガルーダが送り込む敵を次々と退けたコン・バトラーＶは、アンドロイドであったガルーダを作り上げた女帝オレアナを打倒する。
   しかし、オレアナの死により、新たにキャンベル星から女帝ジャネラ、総統ワルキメデス、将軍ダンゲルらが地球に送り込まれる。
   キャンベル星で平和主義者による反乱が起きる中、コン・バトラーＶはジャネラとの決戦に勝利し、地球に平和をもたらすのであった。

   EN: Aliens from Planet Campbell have been lurking deep inside the Earth since prehistoric times. As the new millennium approached, they were ready to begin their invasion of the planet.
   
   One man anticipated these events: Dr. Takeshi Nambara. He decided to stand against the Campbellian menace by developing five machines that would combine into a single, giant robot through the power of superelectromagnetism: Combattler V. He then selected five people from all over Japan who would form the Battle Team: Hyoma Aoi, Juzo Naniwa, Daisaku Nishikawa, Kosuke Kita, as well as his own granddaughter Chizuru Nambara.
   
   However, Dr. Nambara was critically injured during one of the early Campbellian assaults, entrusting the battle against Campbell to his best friend, Dr. Yotsuya, before passing away.
   
   After suffering repeated losses, the Great General Garuda of Campbell eventually discovered the truth: he was not the son of the Empress Oleana, but in fact an android she created. Feeling betrayed, Garuda killed Oleana, and then challenged Combattler V to mortal combat. In the end, the Battle Team stood victorious.
   
   However, after Oleana's demise, new threats came from Planet Campbell: the Empress Janera and her followers, Minister Warchimedes and General Dungele.
   
   Ultimately, Combattler V defeated Janera, and with Planet Campbell being led by a new pacifist faction, the war was over: Earth had earned its peace.

1528. [Nazwa własna] 宇宙世紀００８７年。
   地球連邦軍がジオン公国軍と戦い、勝利した一年戦争から７年の月日が流れていた。
   連邦軍内で新たに台頭したエリート部隊・ティターンズは、コロニーや月面に住むスペースノイドへの弾圧を強行していた。
   勢力を拡大させるティターンズに反発する者達は、反地球連邦組織・エゥーゴを結成。その中には、かつてのジオン公国のエースパイロットであり、連邦に「赤い彗星」と呼ばれて恐れられたシャア・アズナブルもクワトロ・バジーナと名を変えて、参加していた。
   エゥーゴがティターンズの拠点であるコロニー・グリーンオアシスを襲撃した際、その争いに巻き込まれた少年カミーユ・ビダンは、ティターンズへの反発からエゥーゴに参加。パイロットとしてティターンズとの戦いに身を投じたカミーユは、ニュータイプとしての素質を開花させていく。
   戦局はエゥーゴとティターンズとの戦いにジオンの残党であるアクシズが加わった事で、三つ巴となっていった。
   状況を打破するためにエゥーゴは、制圧したコロニーレーザーの使用を画策する。
   その照射を阻止せんとするティターンズのパプテマス・シロッコと、アクシズのハマーン・カーンを相手にカミーユとクワトロは激闘をくり広げる。
   結果、コロニーレーザーは発射され、後に「グリプス戦役」と呼ばれるこの戦いは、大きな犠牲を払いながらもエゥーゴの勝利に終わる。
   しかし、ハマーンとの戦いの中でクワトロは行方不明となり、カミーユもシロッコとの激闘に勝利したものの、シロッコの最後の執念により精神を崩壊させてしまうのであった。

   EN: Universal Century, Year 0087.
   
   7 years have passed since the One Year War, in which the Earth Federation Forces fought and emerged victorious against the troops of the Principality of Zeon. A new elite force within the Federation called the Titans now oppresses the Spacenoids living in the colonies and on the Moon's surface.
   
   Those who oppose the Titans' expanding power have established the Anti Earth United Government, or AEUG. It includes ace pilots from the former Principality, and the feared Char Aznable, known as the "Red Comet", has joined under the alias "Quattro Bajeena".
   
   When AEUG attacks the Titans' base Colony Green Oasis, teenager Kamille Bidan is caught up in the battle and joins the AEUG rebellion against the Titans. Kamille throws himself into the fight as a pilot, and his natural talent as a Newtype blossoms.
   
   A third group, the Zeon remnant faction Axis, eventually enters the war between AEUG and the Titans. AEUG schemes to use the powerful Colony Laser to gain an advantage in the war. Kamille and Quattro wage a fierce fight against Titans' Paptimus Scirocco and Axis' Haman Karn, who are trying to prevent the plan from coming to fruition. In the end, the Colony Laser is fired and AEUG emerges victorious, but this act also results in a large number of casualties. This war came to be known as the Gryps Conflict.
   
   Quattro goes missing in action during a fight with Haman, and even though Kamille defeats Scirocco, he is psychologically broken by Scirocco's final blow.
   PL:

1529. [Nazwa własna] 宇宙世紀００９３年。
   グリプス戦役以降、行方不明となっていたシャア・アズナブルがネオ・ジオンの総帥に就任した。
   宇宙移民を抑圧して地球にしがみつく人類を粛清するため地球連邦に宣戦布告したシャアは、小惑星５ｔｈルナを地球に落下させようとする。アムロの所属する連邦軍の外郭部隊ロンド・ベルは、その阻止に奮戦するも、５ｔｈルナは地球に落下してしまう。
   その後、シャアは地球政府高官と裏取引を行い、ネオ・ジオンの武装解除を条件に、かつてのネオ・ジオンの小惑星要塞アクシズを手に入れる。しかし、シャアはその協定を破り、今度はアクシズを地球に衝突させるべく行動を開始する。
   その作戦を阻止するため、そしてシャアとの決着をつけるため、アムロは最新鋭モビルスーツ・νガンダムに搭乗し、アクシズを目指す。
   アクシズ上で激突するアムロとシャア。
   アムロは勝利するものの、ロンド・ベルは地球に落下していくアクシズの破壊に失敗する。
   既に大気圏に突入しつつあったアクシズを食い止めるため、アムロはνガンダムでアクシズに取りつき、これを押し返そうとする。
   νガンダムから発せられたサイコ・フレームの光はアクシズの落下を防ぎ、その温かな光は目撃した人達の心に何かを残していく。
   この戦いでアムロとシャアは共に行方不明となり、歴史から姿を消すのであった。

   EN: Universal Century, Year 0093.
   
   After the Gryps Conflict, Char Aznable, who had been missing in action, is appointed commander of Neo Zeon. He declares war on the Earth Federation, wanting to purge those that clung to Earth and held back the Space Colonies. He plans to drop an Asteroid called 5th Luna on Earth. Special force Londo Bell of the Earth Federation, of which Amuro is a member, fights to thwart his plans. It is in vain and 5th Luna eventually collides with Earth.
   
   Later, Char makes a secret agreement with the Earth Federation government officials, in which the asteroid stronghold Axis would be transferred to Neo Zeon on the condition that Neo Zeon disarms.
   
   However, Char breaks the agreement and prepares to launch Axis towards Earth.
   
   Amuro pilots the cutting-edge Mobile Suit Nu Gundam on a course for Axis to stop Char's plan and finally bring an end to the conflict.
   
   After a fierce battle with Char on Axis, Amuro emerges victorious, but Londo Bell fails to destroy Axis as it plummets to Earth.
   
   As Axis enters the Earth's atmosphere, Amuro uses the Nu Gundam to stop and attempt to push it back. The Nu Gundam's Psycho-frame prevents Axis colliding with Earth in a glorious flash of light which leaves a lasting an impression on all who witnessed it.
   
   Amuro and Char go missing after the battle, never to be seen again...

1530. [Nazwa własna] 未来世紀。
   荒廃した地球に見切りをつけた人類は、宇宙にスペースコロニー国家を作り、そこで生活していた。
   また各国は全面戦争を避けるため、地球をリング（戦場）に各国代表のガンダムを戦わせ、それを勝ち抜いた国に四年間の政治主導権を与えるガンダムファイトを開催していた。
   そして未来世紀６０年、ネオジャパンはガンダムファイト第１３回大会にガンダムファイター、ドモン・カッシュを送り込んだ。幼なじみでありクルーでもあるレインと共に各地を旅するドモンは、各国のガンダムと戦いながら、最強最悪の兵器、デビルガンダムに搭乗して地球へ逃亡した兄キョウジを追っていた。
   その旅の中、ドモンはライバル達と拳を交える事で互いを認め合い、かけがえのない友情を築いていった。
   また、師匠にして前大会の優勝者である東方不敗と再会を果たすが、東方不敗はデビルガンダムと結託しており、人類の敵として立ちはだかる。
   苛烈を極めた戦いの中、ドモンはついにデビルガンダムを打ち倒す。さらに東方不敗との激闘を制し、第１３回ガンダムファイトの覇者となる。
   全ての決着がついたと思われたが、ネオジャパンのウルベの策略によって、レインを生体コアにしてデビルガンダムが復活してしまう。さらにデビルガンダムは周辺のコロニーまでも吸収し、以前よりも強大な姿に成長をとげる。しかし、愛するレインを救い出したドモンにより、デビルガンダムはコアを破壊され完全に消滅する。
   世界に平和を取り戻したガンダムファイター達は、４年後の第１４回大会での再会を誓い合うのであった。

   EN: The Future Century.
   
   Having given up on their ruined planet, humanity forsakes Earth to live inside colonies out in space.
   
   To avoid war among the colony nations, a competition is held every four years. One representative from each nation pilots a Gundam and takes part in a tournament called the "Gundam Fight", where Earth is the ring. The pilots fight until the nation represented by the last standing Gundam earns the right to rule all the colony nations.
   
   In year 60 of the Future Century, Neo Japan sends Domon Kasshu as its 13th Gundam Fight representative. Traveling with Rain, his childhood friend and crew, Domon fights against the Gundams of the other nations while searching for his older brother Kyoji, who has fled to Earth with the most powerful and evil weapon, the Dark Gundam.
   
   Through the exchange of fists, Domon and his rivals come to understand each other, forming an irreplaceable bond of friendship.
   
   Domon also reunites with Master Asia, his teacher and winner of the previous Gundam Fight. However, his master has sided with the Dark Gundam and is now an enemy of humanity.
   
   After an intense battle, Domon finally defeats the Dark Gundam. He also bests Master Asia, emerging as the champion of the 13th Gundam Fight.
   
   Everything seems to be over until Urube of Neo Japan kidnaps Rain, forcing her to act as the Dark Gundam's bio-unit. Furthermore, the Dark Gundam absorbs the surrounding colonies, becoming stronger than ever. However, Domon is able to rescue his beloved Rain. with its core destroyed, the Dark Gundam disintegrates.
   
   Having restored peace to the world, the Gundam Fighters vow to meet again four years later, at the 14th Gundam Fight.
   PL:

   EN: A.C. 196, Christmas.
   
   One year has passed since the end of the battle of Operation Meteor that began with 5 Gundams descending to Earth, and the world is on the road to peace.
   
   The Gundam pilots are now part of the Preventers, an intelligence agency run by Lady Une. They track the activities of an organization known as the Mariemaia Army, led by a young girl named Mariemaia. She claims to be the daughter of Treize Khushrenada, the now-deceased ruler of the world's countries, and has gathered strength in secret along with the head of Barton Foundation, Dekim Barton. Wufei, the Gundam pilot, is also with her.
   
   The Mariemaia Army kidnaps Vice Foreign Minister Relena Darlian, the symbol of peace, declaring war on the world, and sets True Operation Meteor to drop a colony onto Earth into action.

1532. [Nazwa własna] ヒイロ達ガンダムパイロットとリリーナの兄・ゼクス・マーキスの活躍によってコロニーの落下作戦は阻止されたものの、その隙をついてマリーメイア軍は大規模なモビルスーツ部隊を地上へと降下させ、大統領府を制圧する。
   後を追って降下したゼクス、ノイン、そしてガンダムパイロットのデュオ、カトル、トロワはパイロットを殺さない不殺の戦いを行うも、圧倒的な物量差に苦戦を強いられる。
   そこへ到着したヒイロは、五飛との戦いで半壊したウイングガンダムゼロを駆り、ツインバスターライフルで大統領府を守るシェルターを狙撃し、機体を大破させながらもリリーナを救出すべく内部への侵入を成功させる。
   ヒイロの攻撃に対して初めて戦いの恐怖を知り、自らの過ちを認識したマリーメイアは、リリーナをかばってデキムの凶弾に倒れる。
   しかし、デキムのその行動は兵士達の怒りを買い、デキムは逆賊として味方の兵士に射殺され、戦いは終結する。
   戦争がなくなった世界に不要となったガンダムは、パイロットの手によって破壊され、彼らは各々の道を歩み始めるのであった。

   EN: Heero and the other Gundam pilots, along with Relena's brother Zechs Merquise, successfully stop the fall of the colony. The Mariemaia Army takes advantage of that gap to send a large Mobile Suit squadron down to Earth, taking over the Presidential Office. Zechs, Noin, and the Gundam pilots Duo, Quatre, and Trowa follow after. They fight without killing, but are forced into a close battle due to the sheer difference in resources.
   
   Heero arrives in his Wing Gundam Zero, already half-destroyed during the fight against Wufei. He uses his Twin Buster Rifles to attack the unit guarding the Presidential Office, and succeeds in breaking through in order to save Relena, despite great damage to his unit. Mariemaia learns of the horrors of war after witnessing Heero's attacks, and realizes her own mistake. Dekim fires a bullet, and Mariemaia takes it in order to protect Relena. However, Dekim's actions angered their forces and he is branded a traitor. He is killed by his own forces, bringing an end to the fighting.
   
   With the end of all fighting, Gundams no longer have a place in the world. The Gundams are destroyed by Heero, and each of them continue down their own path in life.

1533. [Nazwa własna] コズミック・イラ（Ｃ．Ｅ）７１年。
   遺伝子操作された新人類「コーディネイター」と遺伝子操作されていない通常の人類「ナチュラル」との確執から起こった地球連合軍とプラントの争いの中、地球連合軍は中立国であるオーブに侵攻する。民間人にも多大な被害をもたらしたこの戦いで、シン・アスカは両親と妹を失う。
   それから２年。ザフトに入隊したシンは、新型モビルスーツ・インパルスガンダムのパイロットとなり、強奪された３機の新型モビルスーツの奪還作戦に参加する。また、その任務を与えられた戦艦ミネルバにはプラント最高評議会議長ギルバート・デュランダルと２年前の戦いの英雄アスラン・ザラも乗船していた。
   その任務の最中、シン達はナチュラル根絶を訴えるザフトの脱走兵達が起こしたユニウス・セブン落下事件に遭遇する。ミネルバの活躍により、ユニウス・セブンは分断されたものの破片は地上に降り注ぎ、各地に多大な被害をもたらす。
   それが原因となり、再び地球連合とプラントとの争いが勃発する。
   戦いが激化する中、前大戦の英雄、キラ・ヤマトも戦いに介入し、戦局は混迷の一途をたどる。
   次第にデュランダルを盲信していくシンは、アスランと反目し、さらに戦いを止めようとするキラを撃破する。
   一方、デュランダルは人類を適正に管理するシステム「デスティニープラン」を提唱。人の運命を管理するその構想に反対するキラやアスランは、デュランダル率いるザフト艦隊やシンと激突する。
   最後の戦いでシンはアスランに破れ、デュランダルも死亡し、人類の未来と自由は守られるのであった。

   EN: Year 71 of the Cosmic Era.
   
   During a war between the Earth Alliance Forces and PLANT, brought about by the feuding between the Coordinators—the new, genetically-enhanced humanity—and the Naturals—normal humans without genetic modification—the Earth Alliance invades Orb, a neutral nation. The conflict takes the lives of many civilians, including Shinn Asuka's parents and younger sister.
   
   Two years later, Shinn, having joined ZAFT, becomes the pilot of a new Mobile Suit called the Impulse Gundam. With the battleship Minerva, he takes part in a mission to capture three other new Mobile Suits stolen from ZAFT. Also on board the Minerva is PLANT Supreme Council Chairman Gilbert Dullindal and Athrun Zala, a hero from the war two years prior.
   
   During their mission, Shinn and the others encounter Junius Seven, which is being pulled down by ZAFT deserters advocating the eradication of all Naturals. Through the Minerva's actions, Junius Seven is split into pieces; however, those fragments still rain down on Earth, causing massive damage and casualties all over the world.
   
   The incident causes a second war to break out between the Earth Alliance and PLANT.
   
   As the fighting intensifies, Kira Yamato, another hero from the previous war, intervenes, bringing even more chaos to the conflict.
   
   Shinn, his blind faith in Dullindal gradually growing, rebels against Athrun and goes on to defeat Kira, who tried to stop further warfare.
   
   Meanwhile, Dullindal announces his Destiny Plan, a system to properly supervise humankind. Kira and Athrun are in opposition to the way it would control people's fates, and clash with the ZAFT fleets led by Dullindal, as well as with Shinn.
   
   In the final battle, Shinn loses to Athrun, Dullindal perishes, and the future and freedom of mankind are preserved.

   EN: 2067 A.D.
   
   It is an age where Earthnoids have spread across the stars, driven by a galactic colonization plan. One of these colonized areas, the Brisingr Globular Cluster was suddenly struck by a strange disease that turned people berserk: Vár Syndrome.
   
   In response, the interstellar conglomerate Xaos (pronounced "chaos") established the Tactical Sound Unit Walküre, whose songs were able to quell outbreaks of Vár Syndrome. Escorted on their duties by Delta Squadron, in their transforming VF-31 Siegfried variable fighters, Walküre held live concerts across the region.
   
   One young woman, Freyja Wion, was going to attend an audition to join Walküre, who she was greatly inspired by. While traveling to the audition, she instead found herself in the midst of a Vár Syndrome outbreak, where she would meet Delta Flight's newest pilot, Hayate Immelmann.
   
   In the thick of this chaos, she was able to calm the outbreak with her singing. As such, she was recognized for her abilities and joined Walküre, standing with Hayate in the battle against Vár Syndrome...and what ultimately caused it.

1548. [Nazwa własna] 皇暦２０１０年。
   超大国「神聖ブリタニア帝国」は日本へ侵攻し、人型兵器「ＫＭＦ（ナイトメアフレーム）」による圧倒的な戦力差でその全土を属領とした。
   「エリア１１」と呼ばれた日本に住むルルーシュは、謎の少女Ｃ．Ｃ．と契約し、他人に命令を強制する力を持つ「ギアス」を得る。
   ブリタニア帝国の第１１皇子でありながら、政治の道具として利用した父である皇帝シャルルに憎しみを抱くルルーシュは、天才的な知略とギアスの力を使い、仮面の男「ゼロ」としてレジスタンス組織「黒の騎士団」を創設。日本独立を掲げ、妹ナナリーの平穏と自らの復讐のためにブリタニア帝国に対して反逆を開始する。
   かつてのルルーシュの親友で今は帝国の一員の枢木スザクが立ちふさがるも、協力者達を集めて勢力を拡大するゼロは、連合国家「超合集国」を発足。
   やがてルルーシュは、父シャルルと意識体となった母マリアンヌの真の目的を知り、世界を作り変えようとするその野望を砕き、自らの復讐を終える。
   全てを知って同志となった枢木スザクとＣ．Ｃ．らの協力の下、ルルーシュはブリタニア帝国を乗っ取り、皇帝ルルーシュ・ヴィ・ブリタニアとして即位。
   ブリタニアの体制を全て破壊し、黒の騎士団と手を組んだシュナイゼルを倒して世界を征服後、世界中の憎しみを集めた。そして、世界中の人達が注目するパレードで、ゼロに扮したスザクに討たれる。
   世界征服からゼロ（＝スザク）による粛清までの全ては、ルルーシュが自らの命を犠牲とした計画「ゼロ・レクイエム」であった。悪の象徴となったルルーシュが討たれた事で、世界は憎しみの連鎖を断ち切り新しい時代を迎えた。
   ゼロ・レクイエムから１年後の光和２年。世界は超合集国を中心に再編成され、平和な時が流れていた。しかし、難民キャンプの視察に訪れていた世界人道支援機関名誉顧問ナナリーと黒の騎士団の首席顧問ゼロ（＝スザク）が、正体不明の武装勢力に拉致されるという事件が発生。
   事件解決のため、「戦士の国」と呼ばれるジルクスタン王国に潜入したカレン達は、そこで謎のギアス使いに襲撃される。
   その場には、命を落としたはずのルルーシュとＣ．Ｃ．の姿があった。

   EN: The date was August 10, in the year 2010 of the Imperial Calendar.
   
   The superpower known as the Holy Britannian Empire had declared war on Japan. In an overwhelming display of power, they quickly conquered the entire nation using their humanoid weapons, the Knightmare Frames (or KMFs).
   
   Lelouch, a young man living in Area 11 (the area formerly known as Japan), made a contract with the mysterious woman C.C., receiving the power of absolute control over others: the Geass.
   
   Lelouch was actually the 11th crown prince of Britannia, but he hated being used as a political tool; thus, he decided to combine his genius intellect with the power of the Geass.
   
   He became the masked man "Zero" and founded the "Black Knights", a resistance organization fighting against Britannia. Though publicly he was fighting to free Japan from the oppressive rule of the Empire, in truth he was only trying to create a peaceful world for his sister Nunnally, as well as achieve his personal vengeance.
   
   While Suzaku Kururugi, once a close friend of Lelouch, stood in his way as a soldier of Britannia, the Black Knights managed to establish the "United Federation of Nations", countries joined together in their opposition to the Empire.
   
   Eventually, Lelouch learned the true objective of his father Charles and his mother Marianne: mercilessly reshaping the world into a place without lies. He then crushed their ambition, thus achieving his vengeance.
   
   With the help of Suzaku Kururugi and C.C., the only people in the world who knew the truth of what would be happening, Lelouch ascended to the throne of the Britannian Empire as Emperor Lelouch vi Britannia. After tearing down the original Britannian government and defeating the combined efforts of the Black Knights and Schneizel, Lelouch managed to conquer the entire world. He then proceeded to act like the worst of tyrants, gathering all the hatred of the people of the world onto him. Finally, during a parade broadcast to the entire world, he was killed by Zero (this time with Suzaku wearing the costume).
   
   Everything, from world conquest to being killed by Zero, was part of Lelouch's final gambit: the Zero Requiem. With the death of the "Evil Emperor Lelouch", the world would finally enter a new era, free from hatred.
   
   And then...Year 2 of the Kowa era. It had been one year since the Zero Requiem.
   
   Thanks to the reorganized United Federation of Nations, the world was celebrating an unprecedented time of peace. However, Nunnally, honorary advisor for the World Humanism Agency, and Zero (actually Suzaku), chief counselor of the Order of the Black Knights, were kidnapped by a mysterious armed group while visiting a refugee camp. In order to solve the case, Kallen and her team infiltrated the Kingdom of Zilkhstan, known as the Nation of Warriors, where they were attacked by a mysterious Geass user.
   
   That's when they happened upon C.C., who was traveling with none other than Lelouch, who was supposed to be dead...
   PL:

1549. [Nazwa własna] カレン達の協力を得て潜入したジルクスタンの大監獄で、ルルーシュはＣの世界から意識を取り戻す。ルルーシュは囚われていたスザクを救出し、かつての仲間を率いて大監獄を脱出後、ナナリー救出のための作戦を決行する。
   ルルーシュはジルクスタンの指導者である聖神官シャムナを追い詰めるが、彼女の時を遡るギアス・無限新生により時間は巻き戻される。
   シャムナのギアスによって全ての策を見抜かれ、作戦を封じられるルルーシュだったが、Ｃ．Ｃ．に叱咤され、膨大な作戦プランからシャムナの能力を絞り込み、逆に彼女を追い込む。
   ルルーシュは、自国のために世界を再び戦火に包もうとしたシャムナを否定し、彼女を永遠に眠らせた。
   Ｃの世界からナナリーの意識を救出して全てを終えたルルーシュは、かつての仲間達の下を去り、Ｃ．Ｃ．と共にギアスの欠片を探す旅に出るのであった。
   limit: do 1139 znaków
   EN: With the help of Kallen, they were able to infiltrate the Zilkhstan Prison and allow Lelouch to reclaim his consciousness from C's World. He then proceeded to rescue Suzaku, and lead his former comrades in a rescue operation to save Nunnally.
   
   Lelouch went after Shamna the Divine, the priestess of Zilkhstan, but each time he came close to defeating her, time rewound due to Shamna's Geass power of endless rebirth.
   
   At first, Shamna used her Geass to "predict" every single one of Lelouch's plans, almost bringing him to a breaking point; it was only thanks to C.C.'s heartfelt rebuke that Lelouch returned to his former self, allowing him to come up with a grand plan that would force Shamna to reveal her power, and turn it back against her.
   
   Once he came face to face with her, Lelouch rejected Shamna's wish to create a world of endless conflict for the sake of her warrior nation, and used his own Geass to let her sleep for eternity.
   
   After rescuing Nunnally's consciousness from C's World, Lelouch bid farewell to his old friends and left on a journey alongside C.C. in order to look for Geass fragments throughout the world.

1550. [Nazwa własna] 地球歴２１１０年。
   宇宙に進出した人類は、木星圏にて汎銀河統一帝国を名乗る謎の勢力ウルガルの襲撃に遭い、存亡の危機に直面していた。

   EN: 2110 A.D.
   
   Mankind's advance into outer space was halted when they reached the Jupiter Sphere, and fell under the attack of the United Pan-Galactic Empire Wulgaru.
   
   Meanwhile on Earth, the military organization MJP was moving forward with a project to breed genetically altered humans better suited to operate in space.
   
   Izuru Hitachi was part of Team Rabbits, a five-man group affiliated with MJP. Individually, they were highly talented; however, their inability to work together as a team had earned them the moniker of "the Fail Five".
   
   Even so, they were ordered to board the state-of-the-art Combat Devices known as AHSMB (pronounced "Ashemby"), and were sent to fight against the Wulgaru.

1551. [Nazwa własna] 慣れないながらも何とか戦い続けるチームラビッツは、ウルガルから亡命してきた皇女テオーリアから彼等の真の目的を聞く。それは種族存続のために優秀な遺伝子を回収する事であり、その戦いは彼等にとって「狩り」と呼ばれる行為であった。
   後輩のアンジュを加えて戦い続けるチームラビッツであったが、イズルは宿敵ジアートとの激戦を経て身体に異常をきたし、ついには出撃を禁じられる。ウルガルの太陽系侵攻の通路となる次元移送ゲート破壊作戦の中、イズルは不利な戦況を覆すため、また仲間を守るために出撃する。
   激闘の末、ＭＪＰのシモン司令による宇宙ステーション・スターローズの突撃でゲートは破壊され、真の覚醒を果たしたイズルはジアートに勝利する。全てを終えたイズルは、ヒーローとして仲間達に迎え入れられるのであった。
   limit: do 1134 znaków
   EN: Despite their paucity of initial experience, Team Rabbits continued to fight and rack up victories. Eventually, they learned the true objective of the Wulgaru from a defector, Princess Teoria.
   
   Apparently, for the Wulgaru, the entire conflict was but a "hunt". Their goal was to recover superior genetic material to ensure the survival of their own species.
   
   As the war continued, Ange Kuroki was added to the members of Team Rabbits; however, after a series of battles with the enemy commander Jiart, Izuru's health began to deteriorate, and was thus forbidden from further combat operations.
   
   During one of the operations to destroy the Wulgaru's dimensional gate, their path into the Solar System, Izuru ignored his orders and deployed into battle again in order to protect his friends.
   
   After a fierce battle, the gate was finally destroyed when MJP Commander Simon set the space station Star Rose on a collision course with the gate; meanwhile, Izuru achieved his true awakening in order to defeat Jiart.
   
   Ultimately, Izuru survived the confrontation, and was welcomed back by his friends as the hero he always wanted to be.

   EN: Nigashio, Chiba prefecture, 2030.
   
   Yun Arikawa, an engineer working for a car and robotics garage called the Otaki Factory, investigates a mansion on a deserted island due to reports of hauntings. Meanwhile, graduate student Mei Kamino's research on imaginary creatures leads her to a mysterious radio transmission from the old Misakioku city center.
   
   Each of them, working separately and in different locations, hear the same song and find themselves facing a series of kaiju phenomena that threaten to change the world as we know it. 1557. [Nazwa własna (DLC1)] 太陽系全域に人類が進出した時代。

   「かみそり」の異名を持つアイザックは、ブラスター・キッド、飛ばし屋・ボウィー、エンジェル・お町と共に、莫大な報酬と引き換えに悪を討つ始末屋「コズモレンジャーＪ９」を結成する。
   彼等はブライシンクロン理論によって変形・巨大化するブライガーを駆り、星々を股に掛け、様々な敵と戦っていく。
   そして、姿を現す最大の敵、ヌビア・コネクションのカーメン・カーメン。
   Ｊ９は、太陽系を造りかえるカーメンの野望「大アトゥーム計画」に立ち向かっていく。

   EN: Humanity has spread throughout the solar system, but the insidious hand of the criminal organization Connection also spreads in the shadows of society.
   
   Isaac the Razor, together with Blaster Kid, Speedy Bowie, and Angel Omachi have formed the Cosmo Rangers J9, a troubleshooter organization that eliminates evil in exchange for hefty rewards.
   
   In Braiger, which can change form and grow massive thanks to the Brai-synchron principle, they travel from star to star facing various enemies. Standing against them is Khamen Khamen, head of the Nubia Connection and the greatest of their foes.
   
   It is up to J9 to stop Khamen Khamen's evil plan to reshape the solar system through the Great Atum Project.
   PL:

1558. [Nazwa własna (DLC1)] 左翔太郎は、探偵の師匠である鳴海荘吉との出会いと別れについて語る。
   かつて、風の街「風都」でガイアメモリを使う怪人・ドーパントが暗躍し始めた頃、少年・翔太郎は荘吉の背中を追い、探偵助手として働いていた。
   荘吉は探偵稼業の傍ら、仮面ライダースカルとして秘密裏に活動していたが、その彼の下に一本の依頼が入る。
   依頼を受けた荘吉は、謎の組織「ミュージアム」の施設から少年を救出し、フィリップと名付けるも、凶弾に倒れてしまう。
   依頼人からの「切り札」を荘吉に届けるため、その跡を追っていた翔太郎は失意の底で出会ったフィリップと共に仮面ライダーＷに変身し、荘吉の意志を受け継ぐのであった。
   そして彼等は、荘吉の娘・鳴海亜樹子と共に鳴海探偵事務所を引き継ぎ、風都の平和を守って戦っている。

   EN: Shotaro's story of how he first met—and ultimately lost—his mentor, Sokichi Narumi.
   
   As Dopants—monsters using the powers of Gaia Memories—began to spread through the underbelly of the Windy City of Fuuto, a young Shotaro grew up trying to follow Sokichi's example, ultimately becoming his assistant.
   
   Sokichi ran his detective agency while secretly fighting as Kamen Rider Skull, until a fateful case came to him. He was tasked with rescuing a boy from the organization known as the Museum. He gave the boy the name Phillip, but Sokichi was shot dead before he could make his escape.
   
   However, Shotaro had followed him, hoping to bring him the "Joker" supplied by the client. In Sokichi's final moments, he passed on his business to Shotaro, and together with Philip, the pair would fulfil his wish. At this low point of Shotaro's life, he transforms into Kamen Rider Double with Philip.
   
   Sokichi's daughter Akiko joins them, so the three carry on his private investigation business, the Narumi Detective Agency, protecting Fuuto from threats both supernatural and everyday.
   PL:

   宙は、自らの意識をコンピュータに移植してマシンファーザーとなった遷次郎、パートナーの美和と共に邪魔大王国を迎え撃つ。

   宙達はマシンファーザーという尊い犠牲を払いながらも竜魔帝王を倒し、平和を守り抜くのであった。

1562. [Nazwa własna (DLC3)] 地球の植民惑星であるソロ星。
   そこで地球人類は古代遺跡から巨大ロボット・イデオンと宇宙船ソロシップを発見する。それは異星人バッフ・クランの伝説に謳われる巨神であった。
   巨神の力を恐れるバッフ・クランの攻撃に対して、コスモ達はイデオンの力で抵抗する。彼等は追撃を逃れるため、ソロシップでソロ星を脱出する。
   誤解と愛憎の果て、そこで生まれた絆や想いも呑み込んで戦いは激化していき、ついには銀河全体を巻き込むまでに拡大していった。
   絶望的な逃避行の中、コスモ達は全てはイデオンに宿る第６文明人の意思「イデ」の導きによるものである事を知る。

   EN: Planet Solo—an Earth colony.
   
   On this distant planet, humanity uncovers the remnants of an ancient civilization: the Solo Ship, a space vessel, and the colossal robot Ideon—the Legendary Giant of Ide spoken of in the folklore of the alien race known as the Buff Clan.
   
   Fearing the power of the legendary giant, the Buff Clan launches a fierce assault on the settlement. Desperate to survive, Cosmo and a group of survivors fight back using Ideon. As the Buff Clan's relentless attacks continue, they have no choice but to flee Planet Solo aboard the Solo Ship. Amid misunderstandings, love, and hatred, the conflict escalates, eventually engulfing the entire galaxy.
   
   During their hopeless search for safety, Cosmo and the other survivors come to realize that they are being guided by the "Ide", the will of the Sixth Civilization that resides within Ideon.
   
   But what fate awaits humanity, at the end of the Ide's trials...?
   PL:

1563. [Nazwa własna (DLC3)] 月崩壊とそれによる大災害「ロスト・アルテミス」から１００年余りが経過した復興歴１０７年。
   隕石除去人型重機「iDOL」とその操縦者「アイドルマスター」は、巨大多国籍企業「モンデンキント」に所属し、地球へと落ちてくる月の欠片（ドロップ）の迎撃に当たっていた。
   新たにインベルのアイドルマスターに選ばれた天海春香は、初めて触れるiDOLにとまどいながらも仲間達と共にiDOL奪取を目論む「トゥリアビータ」との戦いに巻き込まれていく。
   愛憎渦巻く戦いの末、別宇宙へと続くゲート「アウリン」から地球を守るため、春香とインベルは仲間達と共にアウリンへと突入するのであった。

   EN: It is Restoration Year 107, over a hundred years since the destruction of the moon triggered the disaster known as Lost Artemis on Earth.
   
   To protect the planet from the remaining pieces of the moon that fall as Drops, idolmasters pilot their iDOLs, humanoid anti-meteorite machines, under the flag of the massive international corporation Mondenkind.
   
   After being chosen as the idolmaster for Imber, one of the iDOLs, Haruka Amami struggles to find her way as a pilot. Together with her companions she is dragged into battle against Turiavita, an organization seeking to seize control of the iDOLs.
   
   After a conflict that can be described as a whirlpool of hatred and passion, the Earth is faced with a new crisis, as Aulyn, a gate to another dimension, threatens to swallow it. To save the Earth, Haruka pilots the Imber alongside her comrades and dives headfirst into the gate.
   PL:

1564. [Nazwa własna (DLC3)] 日本の南東に浮かぶ南十字島に現れたツナシ・タクト。南十字学園高等部に入学したタクトは、アゲマキ・ワコやシンドウ・スガタと友達になる。
   だが、その裏で秘密結社「綺羅星十字団」のメンバー達は、巨大人型ロボット「サイバディ」を使い、己の野望を果たそうとしていた。
   そして、タクトもまたサイバディ「タウバーン」のスタードライバーであり、サイバディの力を引き出す「銀河美少年」となり、綺羅星十字団に立ち向かっていく。
   サイバディを封印する宿命を持つ四方（よも）の巫女の一人であるワコ、王のサイバディ「キング・ザメク」のスタードライバーであるスガタ、そしてタクトは戦いながらも恋あり夢あり友情ありの青春を過ごす。
   激闘の果て、島の宿命から解放されたワコとスガタと共にタクトは、同じく解放されたサイバディで悪事を働く者達を止めるため、タウバーン・コンパートメントで戦い続ける。

   EN: Takuto Tsunashi arrives on Southern Cross Island, a small island to the southeast of Japan. He begins school at Southern Cross High School and befriends Wako Agemaki and Sugata Shindo.
   
   Unbeknownst to them, the Kiraboshi Order of Cross is conspiring to use the Cybodies, giant robots sealed on the island, for their own ends.
   
   Takuto turns out to be not only the Star Driver of his own Cybody, Tauburn, but a Ginga Bishonen that can draw out their full potential, which he uses to fight against the Order.
   
   As Takuto fights though many battles, he's drawn into a whirlwind of love, adventure, and friendship with Wako, one of the four shrine maidens destined to unseal the Cybodies, and Sugata, the Star Driver of the king Cybody, King Samekh.
   
   Takuto succeeds in freeing Wako and Sugata from being trapped on the island at the end of a long, hard-fought conflict. Afterward, he continues fighting using Tauburn Compartment to prevent other unsealed Cybodies from being used for evil.
   PL:

1565. [Nazwa własna (DLC3)] 高度に進化した情報技術「マナ」を手にした人類は、その魔法のごとき力で豊かな理想郷を手に入れた。
   その世界の大国であるミスルギ皇国の第一皇女・アンジュリーゼも、何不自由なく育ち、１６歳の洗礼の儀も無事終えるはずだった。
   しかし、儀式の最中にアンジュリーゼが、マナの使えない反社会的な人ならざる存在「ノーマ」である事が発覚。
   アンジュリーゼがノーマだとわかった人々は、掌を返して彼女を罵倒し、絶海の孤島アルゼナルに追放する。そこは人類の敵であるドラゴンと戦い続けなければ、生き残れない過酷な地。
   地位も権利も、そして名前すらも奪われ、アンジュリーゼからただの「アンジュ」となった彼女は、人型機動兵器パラメイル・ヴィルキスのメイルライダーとして生きていく事を決意する。
   戦いの中、アルゼナルのメイルライダー達や、古の民の末裔であるタスクと絆を深めていくアンジュ。
   だがその最中、今まで戦ってきたドラゴンが実は並行世界の地球に住む人間・竜の民であることが発覚する。それと共に、アンジュは世界を裏で操り、自らを調律者と名乗るエンブリヲこそが真の敵である事を知る。
   limit: do 1318 znaków
   EN: Humankind has acquired Mana, a highly evolved information technology, and with the magic-like power, they've created a bounteous utopia.
   
   Angelise, first princess of the Empire of Misurugi, one of the world's major powers, was raised without want for anything, and thought her Rite of Baptism on her sixteenth birthday would go off without a hitch. But in the middle of the ceremony, Angelise was revealed to be a Norma, an anti-societal sub-human without the ability to use Mana at all.
   
   Those who found out she was Norma turned on her, exiling her to Arzenal, a solitary island in distant seas—a cruel land where none could survive without keeping up the fight against the Dragons, the enemies of humanity.
   
   Stripped of her position, authority, and even her name, Angelise became simply Ange and resolved herself to living as the Mail Rider of the Villkiss, a Para-Mail humanoid mobile weapon.
   
   During the fighting, she deepened her bond with Tusk, a man born of the union between an Arzenal Mail Rider and a member of the Ancient Folk. However, Ange then discovered that the Dragons they'd been constantly fighting were actually humans known as the Dragon People who lived on a parallel Earth. Ange also discovered her true enemy: Embryo, a man calling himself the Tuner and manipulating the world from the shadows.
   PL:

   EN: Ten years ago, the virtual world inside the Scab Corals collapsed, causing those who lived there to appear on the real Earth in an event known as the Great Merging. Suddenly forced to coexist, the humans from the virtual world and those from the real world—Green Earth and Blue Earth respectively—have developed a deeply ingrained antagonism.
   
   Eureka herself had triggered the Great Merging. Despite losing her beloved Renton, she fought on to atone for what she did to the world.
   During this time, Eureka received orders to protect Iris; a girl who can control Scab Corals like Eureka once could. However, the pair was isolated by Dewey Novak's attacks targeted against Iris and forced to go on the run. Their journey led them to confront their responsibilities and accept who they are.
   PL:

1569. [Nazwa własna (DLC4)] プロスペラの計画に翻弄されるミオリネとスレッタ、そしてスペーシアンとアーシアン。
   スレッタは自身がエアリアルに宿るエリクト・サマヤのリプリチャイルドである事を知り、計画を始動させたプロスペラから切り捨てられる。一方のミオリネは意識不明となった父・デリングの後を継ぐべくベネリットグループ総裁になろうとするが、プロスペラによって宇宙と地球の戦いの火種となってしまい、失意の底へと叩き込まれる。
   しかし、スレッタは自らのやるべきことを見つけ、その真っ直ぐな気持ちに救われたミオリネも再び立ち上がる。
   プロスペラはデータストームの中でしか生きられないエリクトのために巨大要塞「クワイエット・ゼロ」を起動させる。スレッタとミオリネは地球寮の仲間やグエルたちと共にクワイエット・ゼロに向かい、その阻止に成功する。
   ミオリネはベネリットグループの解散を発表し、ガンダムはクワイエット・ゼロと共に消失したが、エリクトは新たな姿を得ることになる。
   ３年後、それぞれが自分の道を進む中、スレッタとミオリネはお互いの手を取り合い、今できることに向けてともに歩いていく。

   EN: Both Miorine and Suletta, as well as the Spacians and the Earthians, are at the mercy of Prospera's plan.
   
   Suletta learns that she is a repli-child of Ericht Samaya, whose consciousness resides within the Aerial. Prospera casts Suletta aside after setting the plan in motion. Miorine tries to become president of the Benerit Group to succeed her unconscious father, Delling. Despite this, Prospera turns Miorine into the catalyst for a conflict between space and Earth, driving her into despair. However, Suletta discovers her purpose, and Miorine, saved by Suletta's sincerity, is able to rise again.
   
   Prospera activates the giant fortress Quiet Zero for Ericht, who can only live within the data storm. Suletta and Miorine, along with their friends from Earth House and Guel's group, head for Quiet Zero and succeed in stopping it.
   
   Miorine announces the dissolution of the Benerit Group. The Gundams vanish along with Quiet Zero, and Ericht takes on a new form.
   
   Three years later, with each of them having followed their own path, Suletta and Miorine take each other's hands and promise to walk forward together toward what they can do now.
